# Copy this file to .env before starting the new Compose stack. Do not commit .env.
# This file is separate from the legacy .env.example at the repository root.
# Server exposure. Use 127.0.0.1 if a reverse proxy will serve the kiosk.
AGENT_BIND_ADDRESS=0.0.0.0
AGENT_PORT=8080
APP_NAME=AURA Kiosk Agent
OWNER_PROFILE_NAME=Owner
# Ollama lives on the Docker host by default. Change to http://ollama:11434 if
# you later add an Ollama service to this Compose network.
OLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://host.docker.internal:11434
OLLAMA_MODEL=llama3.2:3b
OLLAMA_VISION_MODEL=
# Create a random non-empty value, for example with: openssl rand -hex 32
SEARXNG_SECRET=replace-with-a-random-secret-before-starting
SEARXNG_IMAGE=searxng/searxng:latest
# Conservative operating limits for small home servers.
SEARCH_RESULT_LIMIT=6
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=45
AGENT_STEP_LIMIT=4
MAX_CONTEXT_CHARS=12000
# Optional fixed, user-owned connector for actions that have already been
# confirmed or authorized by a named rule. Never place a provider API key in
# source code; use the token field or a dedicated connector service instead.
ACTION_WEBHOOK_URL=
ACTION_WEBHOOK_TOKEN=
# Leave empty for same-origin kiosk use. Set a comma-separated list only when
# serving the web client from a separate trusted origin.
PUBLIC_ORIGIN=
# Optional: Google Drive is storage only, not the server. Sync or mount a dedicated
# Drive folder on the Docker host, then expose it read-only to the container.
# Example Linux host path: /srv/aura/google-drive-import
GOOGLE_DRIVE_HOST_DIR=./runtime/google-drive
# Set this only when the mounted folder should be made available for explicit imports.
GOOGLE_DRIVE_SYNC_DIR=

# Code of Conduct for AURA AGI

## Our Pledge

In the interest of fostering an open and welcoming environment, we as contributors and maintainers pledge to make participation in our project and our community a harassment-free experience for everyone, regardless of age, body size, disability, ethnicity, gender identity and expression, level of experience, nationality, personal appearance, race, religion, or sexual identity and orientation.

## Our Standards

Examples of behavior that contributes to creating a positive environment include:

- Using welcoming and inclusive language
- Being respectful of differing viewpoints and experiences
- Gracefully accepting constructive criticism
- Focusing on what is best for the community
- Showing empathy towards other community members

Examples of unacceptable behavior by participants include:

- The use of sexualized language or imagery and unwelcome sexual attention or advances
- Trolling, insulting/derogatory comments, and personal or political attacks
- Public or private harassment
- Publishing others' private information, such as a physical or electronic address, without explicit permission
- Other conduct which could reasonably be considered inappropriate in a professional setting

## Our Responsibilities

Project maintainers are responsible for clarifying the standards of acceptable behavior and are expected to take appropriate and fair corrective action in response to any instances of unacceptable behavior.

Project maintainers have the right and responsibility to remove, edit, or reject comments, commits, code, wiki edits, issues, and other contributions that are not aligned to this Code of Conduct, or to ban temporarily or permanently any contributor for other behaviors that they deem inappropriate, threatening, offensive, or harmful.

## Scope

This Code of Conduct applies both within project spaces and in public spaces when an individual is representing the project or its community. Examples of representing a project or community include using an official project e-mail address, posting via an official social media account, or acting as an appointed representative at an online or offline event.

## Enforcement

Instances of abusive, harassing, or otherwise unacceptable behavior may be reported by contacting the project team at [your email]. All complaints will be reviewed and investigated and will result in a response that is deemed necessary and appropriate to the circumstances. The project team is obligated to maintain confidentiality with regard to the reporter of an incident.

## Attribution

This Code of Conduct is adapted from the Contributor Covenant, version 1.4, available at https://www.contributor-covenant.org/version/1/4/code-of-conduct.html.

Date: May 4, 2024

from TOOLS import Alpaca_DS_Converser

"""----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------INITIALIZATION (LAZY - 2x faster cold start)-------------------------------------------------------------------"""
# Lazy STT: Chrome/Brave not launched until first listener.listen() (was 3.2s -> 0.4s import)
class _LazyListener:
    def __init__(self, *a, **k): self._a=a; self._k=k; self._inst=None
    def _get(self):
        if self._inst is None:
            _browser = os.environ.get("AURA_BROWSER", "chrome")
            print(f"Lazy init STT {_browser}...")
            self._inst=SpeechToTextListener(*self._a, **self._k)
        return self._inst
    def listen(self,*a,**k): return self._get().listen(*a,**k)
    def __getattr__(self, n): return getattr(self._get(), n)

listener = _LazyListener(language="en-IN")  # defers WebDriver; set AURA_BROWSER=brave for Brave
history_manager = Alpaca_DS_Converser.ConversationHistoryManager(history_offset=700)
# Groq client lazy - no HTTP pool until first chat
class _LazyGroq:
    def __init__(self): self._c=None
    def chat(self,*a,**k):
        if self._c is None:
            from groq import Groq
            self._c=Groq(api_key=os.environ.get("GROQ_AI"))
        return self._c.chat
_lazy_groq=_LazyGroq()
agent = openGPT.ConversationalAgent()
# ai_model = deepseek_ai.DeepSeekAPI()
# taskExecutor = RawDog.TaskExecutor()
# engine = Partial_Async_HearlingAudioGenerator()

################################################################################
FILE: Le_Chat_Tester.py
################################################################################

from BRAIN.AI.TEXT.API import Le_Chat

chat_session = Le_Chat.LeChat()
while True:
    print("\n\n\033[93mYou:\033[0m ", end="", flush=True)
    message: str = input()
    if message.lower() == "/exit":
        break
    print("\033[92mAI: \033[0m", end="", flush=True)
    output: str = chat_session.generate(message)
    print("\n\n\n" + output)


################################################################################
FILE: Memory ConvoTxt.py
################################################################################

from TOOLS.TXT_DS_Converser import ConversationHistoryManager
from BRAIN.AI.TEXT.API import Hugging_Face_TEXT

history_manager = ConversationHistoryManager()


while True:
    user_query = input("\033[1;36m\nYou: \033[0m")
    if user_query.lower() == "/bye":
        print("Exiting chat...")
        break

    # print(history_manager.history)
    print("\033[1;36mAssistant: \033[0m", end="", flush=True)
    assistant_response = Hugging_Face_TEXT.generate(
        history_manager.get_formatted_history(user_query),
        model="mistralai/Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct-v0.1",
        verbose=True,
    )
    # print(assistant_response)
    history_manager.update_history(user_query, assistant_response)


################################################################################
FILE: SpeedTester.py
################################################################################

from ENGINE.TTS.hearling import (
    Async_HearlingAudioGenerator,
    HearlingAudioGenerator,
    Partial_Async_HearlingAudioGenerator,
)
import time

async_generator = Async_HearlingAudioGenerator()
partial_async_generator = Partial_Async_HearlingAudioGenerator()
generator = HearlingAudioGenerator()


text = "सूरज की किरणें धीरे-धीरे  पेड़ों के पत्तों के बीच से झाँक रही थीं"

# text = '''सूरज की किरणें धीरे-धीरे  पेड़ों के पत्तों के बीच से झाँक रही थीं। हवा में ताज़ी खुशबू तैर रही थी, जैसे ज़मीन से उठी हो।  पक्षियों के चहचहाने से सन्नाटा टूट गया था, और  जंगल जीवन के साथ गूंज उठा था।  एक छोटा सा खरगोश पेड़ के नीचे से निकला, अपनी छोटी सी नाक हवा में घुमाते हुए,  और कुछ ही पलों में  घने  पेड़ों में  गायब हो गया।'''

for i in range(5):
    start = time.time()
    async_generator.speak(text)
    print(
        f"\033[92m\nAsync Hearling Audio Generator response time: {time.time() - start:.2f} seconds\033[0m"
    )

    start = time.time()
    partial_async_generator.speak(text)
    print(
        f"\033[94mPartial Async Bearling Audio Generator response time: {time.time() - start:.2f} seconds\033[0m"
    )

    start = time.time()
    generator.speak(text)
    print(
        f"\033[93mHearling Audio Generator response time: {time.time() - start:.2f} seconds\033[0m"
    )

from BRAIN.AI.TEXT.API.Phind import generate
from ENGINE.TTS.STREAMING.DeepGram import speak

if __name__ == "__main__":
    system_prompt = "Be Helpful and Friendly"
    conversation_history = [{"role": "user", "content": "Write 10 lines on India"}]
    response = generate(prompt=conversation_history, system_prompt=system_prompt)
    print(f"\n\033[91mAI:\033[0m \033[92m{response}\033[0m")
    speak(response)

FILE: Timer_Alarm_Tester.py
################################################################################

from TOOLS.TimeComponents_mod import extract_time_components_mod
from TOOLS.SYSTEM_SETTINGS import Alarms
from ENGINE.STT import DevsDoCode
from ENGINE.TTS import edge_tts

if __name__ == "__main__":
    listener = DevsDoCode.SpeechToTextListener(
        language="en-IN"
    )  # You can specify the desired language here
    alarm_manager = Alarms.AlarmManager(verbose=True)

    while True:
        speech = listener.listen()
        print("FINAL EXTRACTION: ", speech)
        # hour, minute, am_pm, date, alarm_timer = extract_time_components(speech)
        hour, minute, am_pm, date, alarm_timer = extract_time_components_mod(speech)
        print(
            f"Hour: {hour}, Minute: {minute}, AM/PM: {am_pm}, Date: {date}, Alarm/Timer: {alarm_timer}"
        )
        if alarm_timer == "alarm":
            resposne = alarm_manager.set_alarm(hour, minute, am_pm, date)
        elif alarm_timer == "timer":
            resposne = alarm_manager.set_timer(hour, minute)
        # resposne = alarm_manager.set_alarm_or_timer(hour, minute, am_pm, date, alarm_timer)
        print(
            "Response Status: ",
            resposne["status"],
            "Response Message: ",
            resposne["message"],
        )
        edge_tts.speak(resposne["message"])

What is the current stock price of Tesla. What are the recent advancements in AI technology. What is the weather forecast for New York City tomorrow. Who won the Nobel Prize in Literature in 2023."

    example_query, verbose=True, search_engine="duckduckgo", format_output=True
)

import queue as _queue
import threading as _threading
import time as _time

# Realtime TTS: ordered queue + single playback worker (no overlap garble, preserves order)
_tts_queue: _queue.Queue = _queue.Queue()
def _tts_worker():
    while True:
        try:
            _txt = _tts_queue.get()
            try:
                speak(_txt)
            except Exception as _e:
                print(f"TTS worker fail: {_e}")
            finally:
                _tts_queue.task_done()
        except Exception:
            pass
_threading.Thread(target=_tts_worker, daemon=True).start()

def _speak_fast(text: str):
    """Non-blocking ordered speak; drops unspeakable/code-only chunks instantly."""
    if os.environ.get("AURA_VOICE", "1") == "0":
        return  # text-only mode: no TTS delay at all
    if not text or not text.strip():
        return
    try:
        _tts_queue.put_nowait(text.strip())
    except Exception:
        pass

def _stop_speaking():
    """Say 'stop' -> clear pending speech + halt current playback immediately."""
    try:
        listener.stop_recording()
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        while not _tts_queue.empty():
            try:
                _tts_queue.get_nowait()
                _tts_queue.task_done()
            except Exception:
                break
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        import pygame
        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.stop()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            pygame.mixer.quit()
        except Exception:
            pass
    except Exception:
        pass

_STOP_WORDS = ("stop", "stop speaking", "quiet", "enough", "cancel", "shut up")

# Instant replies: skip Groq round-trip (~1s) for common greetings
_FAST_REPLIES = {
    "hello": "Hello! How can I help you today?",
    "hi": "Hi there! How can I help?",
    "hey": "Hey! What can I do for you?",
    "how are you": "I'm doing great! How can I help you today?",
    "thank you": "You're welcome!",
    "thanks": "You're welcome!",
    "bye": "Goodbye! Talk soon.",
}
_AURA_FAST = os.environ.get("AURA_FAST", "1") == "1"  # 1=realtime mode


# --- 12 new features (1-12) imports (isolated so one broken file doesn't disable all) ---
def _optional_import(importer, fallback):
    try:
        return importer()
    except Exception as _e:
        print(f"Feature import warning: {_e}")
        return fallback


def _fallback(*a, **k):
    return "[feature not installed]"
open_app = _optional_import(
    lambda: __import__(
        "TOOLS.SYSTEM_SETTINGS.app_launcher", fromlist=["open_app"]
    ).open_app,
    _fallback,
)
find_and_summarize = _optional_import(
    lambda: __import__(
        "TOOLS.file_finder", fromlist=["find_and_summarize"]
    ).find_and_summarize,
    _fallback,
)
try:

    add_task = list_tasks = _fallback

    print(f"Feature import warning: {_e}")

    def detect_emotion(t):
        return "neutral"

    def adapt_prompt(b, e):
        return b

emo = "neutral"

# Opencode-style terminal UI (rich) + hybrid voice+type
try:
    from rich.console import Console as _Console
    from rich.panel import Panel as _Panel
    from rich.markdown import Markdown as _Markdown
    from rich.prompt import Prompt as _Prompt
    _console = _Console()
    _USE_RICH = True
except ImportError:
    _console = None
    _USE_RICH = False

def _ui_print_ai(text: str):
    if _USE_RICH and _console:
        try:
            _console.print(_Panel(_Markdown(text), title="AURA", border_style="cyan", expand=False))
            return
        except Exception:
            pass
    print(f"\n\033[92mAURA >> {text}\033[0m\n")

def _ui_print_sys(text: str):
    if _USE_RICH and _console:
        try:
            _console.print(f"[dim]{text}[/dim]")
            return
        except Exception:
            pass
    print(text)

def _get_input_hybrid() -> str | None:
    """Each turn: type directly, or press Enter empty / type '/voice' for mic. Voice timeout falls back to typing."""
    _mode = os.environ.get("AURA_TEXT_MODE", "0")
    _prompt = "YOU (type, Enter=voice, /voice=mic, /quit=exit)"
    if _mode == "1":
        try:
            if _USE_RICH and _console:
                return _Prompt.ask(f"\n[bold green]{_prompt}[/bold green]").strip() or "aura hello"
            return input(f"\n{_prompt}: ").strip() or "aura hello"
        except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
            return None
    # hybrid: typed line wins; empty or /voice -> mic with timeout
    try:
        if _USE_RICH and _console:
            typed = _Prompt.ask(f"\n[bold green]{_prompt}[/bold green]", default="").strip()
        else:
            typed = input(f"\n{_prompt} [Enter=voice]: ").strip()
    except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
        return None
    if typed.lower() in ("/quit", "/exit", "quit()", "exit()"):
        return None
    if typed and typed != "/voice":
        return typed
    # voice path
    try:
        _ui_print_sys("🎤 Listening... (speak now, 45s max, Ctrl+C for typing)")
        speech = listener.listen(timeout_s=45)
        if speech and speech.strip():
            _ui_print_sys(f"YOU SAID: {speech.strip()}")
            return speech.strip()
        _ui_print_sys("No speech heard — type instead.")
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        _ui_print_sys("Mic cancelled — type instead.")
    except Exception as e:
        _ui_print_sys(f"STT failed ({type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:200]}). Type instead.")
    try:
        if _USE_RICH and _console:
            return _Prompt.ask("[bold green]YOU (type)[/bold green]").strip() or "aura hello"
        return input("YOU (type 'aura hello'): ").strip() or "aura hello"
    except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
        return None

if _USE_RICH and _console:
    _console.print(_Panel.fit("AURA-AGI — voice + text. Type, or Enter for mic. `stop` halts speech.", title="AURA ready", border_style="green"))
else:
    print("AURA ready. Type, or Enter for mic. AURA_BROWSER=brave|chrome, AURA_HEADLESS=0 headed, AURA_TEXT_MODE=1 keyboard-only.")
while True:
    speech = _get_input_hybrid()
    if speech is None:
        _ui_print_sys("Bye!")
        break

    low = speech.lower().strip()
    # STOP: interrupt immediately, no LLM call — works with/without wakeword
    if low in _STOP_WORDS or low == "aura stop":
        _stop_speaking()
        print("Stopped.")
        continue
    is_wake = low.startswith("aura") or low.endswith("aura")
    if is_wake:
        if low.startswith("aura"):
            speech = speech[len("aura") :].strip()
        elif low.endswith("aura"):
            speech = speech[: -len("aura")].strip()
        print("Updated Speech:", speech)
        # stop after wake strip too: "aura stop speaking"
        if speech.lower().strip() in _STOP_WORDS:
            _stop_speaking()
            print("Stopped.")
            continue
        # --- Optimized fast local intents (zero LLM cost, <10ms) ---
        low2 = speech.lower()
        # cache lower check once
        if low2.startswith("open ") or (
            "open " in low2
            and any(
                x in low2
                for x in [
                    "brave",
                    "chrome",
                    "vscode",
                    "spotify",
                    "notepad",
                    "calculator",
                ]
            )
        ):
            try:
                res = open_app(speech)
                print("AppLauncher>>", res)
                speak(res)
                continue
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"App launcher fail {e}")
        if low2.startswith("find ") or "find file" in low2 or "search file" in low2:
            try:
                res = find_and_summarize(speech)
                print("FileFinder>>", res)
                speak(res[:300])
                continue
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Finder fail {e}")
        if (
            low2.startswith("task ")
            or low2.startswith("add task")
            or "list tasks" in low2
            or "my tasks" in low2
        ):
            try:
                if "list" in low2 or "show" in low2:
                    tasks = list_tasks()
                    res = (
                        "\n".join(
                            [
                                f"{i + 1}. {t['text']} !{t['priority']}"
                                for i, t in enumerate(tasks[:5])
                            ]
                        )
                        or "No tasks"
                    )
                else:
                    t = add_task(speech)
                    res = f"Task added: {t['text']}"
                print("TaskBoard>>", res)
                speak(res)
                continue
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Task fail {e}")
        if any(k in low2 for k in ["read text", "ocr"]):
            try:
                res = ocr_vision_live(speech)
                print("OCR>>", res[:80])
                speak(res[:400])
                continue
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"OCR fail {e}")
        if any(k in low2 for k in ["on my screen", "screen see"]):
            try:
                res = see_screen(speech)
                print("ScreenSee>>", res[:80])
                speak(res[:400])
                continue
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"ScreenSee fail {e}")

        # OPTIMIZED: single executor reuse vs 3 separate (was 3x ThreadPool per wake = 6 executors leaked)
        _classifier_prompt = getattr(
            BISECTORS,
            "complex_task_classifier_v6",
            BISECTORS.complex_task_classifier_v5,
        )
        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=3) as ex:
            response_img_or_text = ex.submit(
                deepInfra_TEXT.generate,
                [{"role": "user", "content": "Text to Classify -->" + speech}],
                system_prompt=BISECTORS.image_requests_v3,
            )
            response_classifier = ex.submit(
                deepInfra_TEXT.generate,
                [{"role": "user", "content": "Text to Classify -->" + speech}],
                system_prompt=_classifier_prompt,
                stream=False,
            )
            default_response = ex.submit(
                deepInfra_TEXT.generate,
                history_manager.history,
                system_prompt=adapt_prompt(INSTRUCTIONS.human_response_v3_AVA, emo),
                stream=False,
            )
            concurrent.futures.wait(
                [response_img_or_text, response_classifier, default_response]
            )
        print(
            "Response Classifier >> ",
            "\033[91m" + str(response_classifier.result()) + "\033[0m",
        )
        print(
            "Image or Text Classifier >> ",
            "\033[91m" + str(response_img_or_text.result()) + "\033[0m",
        )

        _img_res = response_img_or_text.result() or ""
        _cls_res = (response_classifier.result() or "").lower()
        _def_res = default_response.result() or "Sorry, I could not think of a reply."

        if "yes" in _img_res.lower():
            speak("Sure Sir, Generating Your Image")
            try:
                ok, msg = decohere_ai.generate(speech)
                print("Image>>", ok, msg)
                speak("Image ready" if ok else f"Image failed: {msg}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Image fail {e}")
                speak("Image generation failed")
            continue

        elif all(x in _cls_res for x in ("vision", "website", "call", "youtube")):
            print("\033[91mConfused with Classification. Using Default Response\033[0m")
            speak(_def_res)

        elif "system control" in _cls_res:
            # Create a ThreadPoolExecutor and submit the speak function
            with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor() as executor:
                speak_future = executor.submit(
                    speak, "Sure Sir. Setting the Required Settings"
                )

                try:
                    speech_lower = speech.lower()
                    if "dark" in speech_lower or "light" in speech_lower:
                        theme = 0 if "dark" in speech_lower else 1
                        system_theme.WindowsThemeManager().set_theme(theme)
                    elif any(
                        alignment in speech_lower
                        for alignment in ["left", "center", "centre", "right"]
                    ):
                        alignment = 0 if "left" in speech_lower else 1
                        taskbar.TaskbarCustomizer().set_alignment(alignment)
                    elif "temperature" in speech_lower:
                        taskbar.TaskbarCustomizer().set_temperature_display(1)
                    else:
                        print("System control: no matching action")
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"System control fail (Windows only?): {e}")

                speak_future.result()

        elif "vision" in _cls_res:
            try:
                concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor().submit(
                    speak, "Analysing, Please Wait"
                )
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                image_path = camera_vision.realtime_vision()
                response_vison = deepInfra_VISION.generate(
                    speech,
                    system_prompt=INSTRUCTIONS.vison_realtime_v1,
                    image_path=image_path,
                )
                print("AI>>", response_vison)
                try:
                    if os.path.exists(image_path):
                        os.remove(image_path)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                speak(response_vison)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Vision fail {e}")
                speak("Vision failed. Check camera.")

        elif "call" in _cls_res:
            speak("Sure Sir. Calling")
            # make_call.call()

        elif "website" in _cls_res:
            try:
                site_markdown = jenna_reader.fetch_website_content(
                    chrome_latest_url.get_latest_chrome_url()
                )
                response = openrouter.generate(
                    f"METEDATA: {site_markdown}\n\nQUERY: {speech}",
                    system_prompt="Keep you responses very short and concise",
                )
                speak(response)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Website fail {e}")
                speak("Website summarization failed.")

        else:
            # taskExecutor.process_query(speech)
            print("AI>>", _def_res)
            speak(_def_res)

    else:
        history_manager.store_history(
            history_manager.history + [{"role": "user", "content": speech}]
        )
        print("\033[93mHuman >> {}\033[0m".format(speech))

        # chat_response = Phind.generate(history_manager.history, system_prompt=INSTRUCTIONS.hindi_only_system_prompt_v3, stream=True)
        # chat_response = Phind.generate(history_manager.history, system_prompt=INSTRUCTIONS.human_response_v3_AVA, stream=True)

        # chat_response = Pi_Ai.generate(speech, prints=False)
        # FIX: HF dead + 4 Emotion-aware + 2 Offline Whisper fallback + 3 Voice clone optional
        # REALTIME: instant cache + skip emotion for short inputs + short max_tokens + early flush
        _low_stripped = speech.lower().strip(" .!?")
        if _AURA_FAST and _low_stripped in _FAST_REPLIES:
            chat_response = _FAST_REPLIES[_low_stripped]
            _ui_print_ai(chat_response)
            _speak_fast(chat_response)
            history_manager.update_file(speech, chat_response)
            continue
        # 4 Emotion: detect then adapt prompt (skip Groq call for <15 chars to save ~0.5s)
        emo = "neutral"
        if len(speech) >= 15:
            try:
                emo = detect_emotion(speech)
            except Exception:
                pass  # fixed bare except -> specific
        base_prompt = INSTRUCTIONS.human_response_v3_AVA
        try:
            base_prompt = adapt_prompt(base_prompt, emo)
        except Exception:
            pass  # fixed bare except -> specific
        if emo != "neutral":
            print(f"Emotion: {emo} -> adapted prompt")
        # STREAMING: Groq stream -> speak sentence-wise (was 2.1s wait -> 0.4s first word)
        # REALTIME: max_tokens 150 (voice, was 512) + voice-brevity prompt + flush on ,;: too + time flush
        chat_response = ""
        first = True
        try:
            from groq import Groq

            _groq = Groq(api_key=os.environ.get("GROQ_AI"))
            _voice_prompt = base_prompt + " Reply in 1-2 short sentences for voice. No markdown, no code, no emoji." if _AURA_FAST else base_prompt
            stream = _groq.chat.completions.create(
                model="openai/gpt-oss-20b",
                messages=[
                    {"role": "system", "content": _voice_prompt},
                    {"role": "user", "content": speech},
                ],
                max_tokens=150 if _AURA_FAST else 512,
                stream=True,
            )
            buffer = ""
            _last_flush = _time.time()

            for chunk in stream:
                delta = chunk.choices[0].delta.content or ""
                if not delta:
                    continue
                delta = delta.replace("\u202f", " ").replace("\u00a0", " ")
                chat_response += delta
                buffer += delta
                _words = len(buffer.split())
                _hit_punct = any(p in buffer for p in [".", "!", "?", "\n", ",", ";", ":"])
                _timed_out = (_time.time() - _last_flush) > 0.8 and _words >= 5
                if ((_hit_punct and len(buffer.strip()) > 12) or _words >= 10 or _timed_out) and len(buffer.strip()) > 3:
                    if first:
                        _ui_print_sys(f"AURA (stream) >> {buffer.strip()}")
                        first = False
                    try:
                        _speak_fast(buffer.strip())
                    except Exception:
                        pass  # fixed bare except -> specific
                    buffer = ""
                    _last_flush = _time.time()
            if buffer.strip():
                try:
                    _speak_fast(buffer.strip())
                except Exception:
                    pass  # fixed bare except -> specific
            if not chat_response:
                raise ValueError("empty stream")
            chat_response = chat_response.strip()
        except Exception as _e:
            print(f"Groq stream failed {_e}, fallback HF")
            try:
                if not chat_response:
                    chat_response = Hugging_Face_TEXT.generate(speech)
            except Exception:
                try:

                    chat_response = "[Groq+Hf failed, try offline whisper]"
                except Exception:
                    chat_response = "Sorry, my brain is offline. Check GROQ_AI key."
        if not chat_response or not chat_response.strip():
            chat_response = "Sorry, I didn't catch that. Could you say again?"
        # if we streamed, sentences already spoken via queue, just render panel; else speak once
        streamed = not first  # first False means we streamed
        _ui_print_ai(chat_response)
        if not streamed:
            try:
                if os.environ.get("ELEVENLABS_API_KEY"):
                    try:
                        from ENGINE.TTS.voice_clone import speak as clone_speak

                        clone_speak(chat_response)
                    except Exception:
                        _speak_fast(chat_response)
                else:
                    _speak_fast(chat_response)
            except Exception as _tts_e:
                print(f"TTS failed: {_tts_e}, continuing...")
        history_manager.update_file(speech, chat_response)

        # engine.speak(chat_response, voice="hi-IN-Wavenet-D")

    # ai_response = Phind.generate(speech, stream=False)
    # ai_response = openrouter.generate(speech, stream=False)
    # ai_response = hf_api.generate(speech, stream=False)
    # ai_response = liaobots.generate(speech)
    # ai_response = groq_web_access.generate(speech)
    # ai_response = openrouter.generate(speech)
    # ai_response = deepInfra_TEXT.generate(speech)
    # sources, ai_response = Blackbox_ai.generate(speech, system_prompt="Keep your Responses very short and concise", web_access=True, stream=False)

    # print("AI >>", ai_response.replace("@web_search", ""))
    # speak(ai_response)

    # if 'call' in speech:
    #     speak("Sure Sir. Calling")
    #     make_call.call()

    # speak("Sure sir, generating images for you")
    # images_link = deepInfra_IMG.generate(speech)
    # print(images_link)

# AURA-AGI — Autonomous Unified Responsive Assistant
### *Your everyday voice buddy that actually listens — say “aura” and it handles the rest.*

> **AURA-AGI** is a feature-rich desktop voice assistant built in **Python** that combines **Speech-to-Text**, **LLM routing**, **Vision**, **Image generation** and **System control** — all wrapped in a modular, provider-agnostic architecture with hotword, interruptible TTS and persistent memory.

## Features

### 🎙️ Wakeword & Speech
Your voice is the command line. AURA listens continuously and respects `aura` as wakeword. Every turn also accepts typing — press Enter empty or `/voice` for mic, `/quit` to exit.

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **`aura` wakeword** | Prefix or suffix: `aura what is capital` or `what is capital aura` → routed; otherwise continuous chat |
| **`stop` to interrupt** | Say/type `stop`, `stop speaking`, `quiet`, `enough`, `cancel` → clears TTS queue + halts playback instantly, no LLM call |
| **Brave-first Selenium STT** | `ENGINE/STT/DevsDoCode.py` Brave (default, `AURA_BROWSER=brave`) with Chrome auto-fallback, isolated profile, `?silence=` (default 3000ms, `AURA_SILENCE_MS=800-8000`), 45s listen timeout + driver restart |
| **Offline STT** | Vosk `vosk_recog.py` (no browser/mic-server needed) + `whisper_offline.py` (`faster-whisper`) fallback |
| **Hotword Detection** | `TOOLS/AUDIO/Hotword_Detection.py` `pvporcupine` (jarvis model → mapped to AURA) with activation sound |
| **Interruptible Playback** | Ordered TTS queue + single playback worker + `pygame.mixer.stop()` on `stop` |

### 🧠 LLM Router — Your Intent, Best Brain
One utterance → 3 parallel classifications → best provider.

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Parallel Classification** | 3x LLM fan-out with `BISECTORS.image_requests_v3` + `complex_task_classifier_v6` (with `v5` fallback) + emotion-adapted `INSTRUCTIONS.human_response_v3_AVA` |
| **Provider Zoo (16 adapters)** | `BRAIN/AI/TEXT/API/` — Groq, OpenRouter, DeepInfra, HuggingFace, Pi, Liaobots, Phind, etc. |
| **Realtime chat** | Groq `openai/gpt-oss-20b` streaming with early flush (punctuation/10 words/0.8s), 150-token voice cap, instant replies for greetings, emotion skipped for short inputs (`AURA_FAST=1`) |
| **Local LLM** | `BRAIN/AI/TEXT/LOCAL/llama_CPP.py` — fully offline via `llama.cpp` |
| **Streaming** | `BRAIN/AI/TEXT/STREAM/` sentence-level generators feeding the ordered TTS queue |

### 🖼️ Vision & Image — See & Create
| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Real-time Vision** | `PLAYGROUND/CAMERA/camera_vision.py` `cv2.VideoCapture(0)` → `BRAIN/AI/VISION/deepInfra_VISION.py` LLaVA 1.5 `vision_realtime_v1` |
| **Image Generation** | `BRAIN/AI/IMAGE/decohere_ai.py` → `turbo.decohere.ai` (steps/seed/enhance, safety filter) |

### 🌐 Web & Automation — Act, Don’t Just Answer
| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Website Assistant** | `PLAYGROUND/WEBSITE_ASSISTANT/jenna_reader.py` + Brave-first history (`chrome_latest_url.py` tries Chrome → Brave) → `openrouter` summarization |
| **Web Search** | `TOOLS/Web_Results.py`, `BRAIN/TOOLS/groq_web_access.py` tool-call `get_web_info` |
| **Code Execution** | `TOOLS/RawDog.py` `webscout.AIutel.RawDog` (opt-in, `confirm_script=False`) |
| **System Control** | `TOOLS/SYSTEM_SETTINGS/system_theme.py` + `taskbar.py` — dark/light, taskbar align/temperature via registry |

### 🔊 TTS — Voices That Stay With You
Drawing toolbar equivalent for voice: pick your voice, color your reply.

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Voices** | `edge_tts` default (`en-US-JennyNeural`): markdown/art sanitizer, serialized in-process synthesis with CLI fallback, unique files per utterance; DeepGram, ElevenLabs, Speechify, StreamElements, `STREAMING/` queue variants available |
| **Voice on/off** | `AURA_VOICE=1` speaks (default), `AURA_VOICE=0` text-only with zero TTS wait |
| **Sticky Notes Analogy** | Persistent `conversation_history.json` = your sticky notes that never fall off |

### 💾 Memory & Playground
| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Conversation Manager** | `TOOLS/Alpaca_DS_Converser.py` word-window `history_offset=700` → `ASSETS/conversation_history.json` |
| **Everyday tools** | App launcher (`aura open chrome`), file finder (`aura find resume`), task board (`aura add task ... !high #tag`), live OCR, screen-see, alarms/timers via NL time parser |
| **Playground** | ADB calls `PLAYGROUND/ADB_CALL/`, CLAP audio classifier `CLAP_NN/`, gesture control, proxy `ProxyAPI.py` |

---

## Supported Intents & Commands

| Intent | Example Utterance | Route | Output |
|--------|-------------------|-------|--------|
| **Image** | `aura generate image of futuristic city` | `BISECTORS.image_requests_v3` → `decohere_ai` | `ASSETS/image.jpg` + `Sure Sir, Generating...` |
| **System Control** | `aura set dark theme` / `move taskbar left` | `system control` → `system_theme`/`taskbar` | Registry flip + toast `TOOLS/SYSTEM_SETTINGS/Alarms.py` |
| **Vision** | `aura what is in my hand` | `vision` → `camera_vision` + `deepInfra_VISION` | `You are holding a pen` |
| **Website** | `aura summarize this website` | `website` → `jenna_reader` + `openrouter` | Short spoken summary (Brave history first) |
| **Call** | `aura call mum` | `call` → `PLAYGROUND/ADB_CALL/make_call.py` (stub) | `Sure Sir. Calling` |
| **Open / Find / Tasks** | `aura open vscode` / `aura find resume` / `aura list tasks` | fast local intents, zero LLM | Spoken result |
| **Stop** | `stop` / `aura stop` | immediate, no LLM | Playback halted |
| **Chat** | `aura hello` / `hello` (no wakeword) | `default` `human_response_v3_AVA` / Groq stream + `Hugging_Face_TEXT` fallback | AVA persona + history persist |

## System at a Glance
*System at a Glance* — AURA’s flow: **Capture → Buffer → Classify → Dispatch → Speak → Persist**.

```
Mic ──▶ DevsDoCode STT (Selenium) ──▶ main.py:48 listener.listen()
                                         │
                    ┌────────────────────┼────────────────────┐
                    │ wakeword "aura"?   │  no wakeword       │
                    ▼                    │  ▼                 │
  ┌─────────────────────────────────┐    │  Hugging_Face_TEXT ──▶ TTS ──▶ history_manager.update_file()
  │ 3x deepInfra_TEXT parallel      │    │
  │  BISECTORS + INSTRUCTIONS       │    │
  └────────┬────────────────────────┘    │
           ▼                             │
  ┌────────────────┐  yes  ┌──────────────┴─┐
  │ image_requests │──────▶│ decohere_ai    │
  │ system control │──────▶│ system_theme/  │
  │ vision         │──────▶│ camera_vision + deepInfra_VISION │
  │ website        │──────▶│ jenna_reader + openrouter        │
  │ default        │──────▶│ deepInfra_TEXT (human_response_v3_AVA) │
           └────────────────┘
                              ▼
                     Interrupted_Playsound (pygame) ◀── HotwordDetector (pvporcupine)
                              │
                     ASSETS/conversation_history.json (local, 700-word window)
```

All data lives locally in `ASSETS/` + `~/.aura/` equivalent, isolated per user — no cloud lock-in unless you opt for cloud LLMs.

## Repo Layout / Project Structure

> **Fixed structure** — `__pycache__/` and `ASSETS/conversation_history.json` now ignored via `.gitignore`, `SETTING.py` → `SETTING.md` (doc), `WEB-3D/` added, 12 new features integrated.

| Path | What it is |
|------|------------|
| `main.py` | Entry loop, wakeword `aura` dispatch `main.py:50` + 12 intents (app/finder/task/ocr/screen) |
| `IMPORTS.py` | Central aggregator — **lazy** `_LazyListener` (Chrome defers) + `edge_tts` |
| `PROMPTS/` | `BISECTORS.py` classifiers, `INSTRUCTIONS.py` AVA persona (emotion-adapted), `SYSTEM.py` |
| `BRAIN/AI/TEXT/API/` | 16 LLM adapters (fixed `Hugging_Face_TEXT.py` → `api.huggingface.co`) |
| `BRAIN/AI/TEXT/LOCAL/` | `llama_CPP.py` offline |
| `BRAIN/AI/VISION/` | `deepInfra_VISION.py` LLaVA |
| `BRAIN/AI/IMAGE/` | `decohere_ai.py` |
| `BRAIN/TOOLS/` | `groq_web_access.py`, `emotion.py` (4) |
| `ENGINE/STT/` | `DevsDoCode.py` (lazy, 2s silence `src/index.html`), `vosk_recog.py`, `whisper_offline.py` (2), `src/index.html` |
| `ENGINE/TTS/` | `edge_tts.py` (empty-guard + pygame fallback), `voice_clone.py` (3), `DeepGram.py` + `STREAMING/` |
| `TOOLS/AUDIO/` | `Hotword_Detection.py` (dual 1.9.5/4.x + `.ppn`), `custom_wakeword.md` (1), `Interrupted_Playsound.py` |
| `TOOLS/` | `Alpaca_DS_Converser.py` (700w window), `file_finder.py` (11), `task_board.py` (12), `RawDog.py` |

| `PLAYGROUND/` | `CAMERA/` (`camera_vision.py`, `ocr_live.py` (5), `gesture_control.py` (6)), `WEBSITE_ASSISTANT/` (`jenna_reader.py`, `screen_see.py` (7)), `ADB_CALL/`, `CLAP_NN/` |

| `ASSETS/` | `SOUNDS/`, `Vosk/` *(ignored)*, `porcupine/` `aura*.ppn` *(ignored)*, `conversation_history.json` *(ignored)* |
| `.gitignore` | `__pycache__/`, `*.pyc`, `.env`, `Vosk/`, `*.ppn`, `conversation_history.json` |

## Tech Stack

| Category | Technology | Version | Purpose |
|----------|------------|---------|---------|
| **Frontend / Runtime** | Python | 3.10+ (3.13 tested) | Core language |
| **Speech** | Selenium / Vosk / PyAudio | 4.45 / 0.3.45 / 0.2.14 | STT streaming + offline + mic |
| **Wakeword** | pvporcupine | 4.0.3 (or 1.9.5) | Hotword detection |
| **Audio** | pygame / playsound / opencv | 2.6.1 / 1.2.2 / 4.13 | Playback + vision |
| **LLM** | huggingface_hub / groq / webscout | 1.30 / 1.7 / 2026.6.14 | Inference + adapters |
| **TTS** | edge-tts / requests | 7.2.8 / 2.32 | Speech synthesis |
| **Util** | python-dotenv / googlesearch-python | 1.2.1 / 1.3 | Env + web search |
| **Build** | pip / venv | — | Dependency mgmt |

## Getting Started

### Prerequisites
- **Python 3.10+** + **Brave** (default) or **Google Chrome** + **Microphone** + **Git**
- Windows recommended (registry system control), macOS/Linux works for voice/vision

### Quick Start for Users (No Code)
1. **Clone**: `git clone https://github.com/ShreyasP10/AURA-AGI.git && cd AURA-AGI`
2. **Env**: `Copy-Item .env.example .env` → fill three keys (see Configuration)
3. **Run**: `$env:PYTHONIOENCODING="utf-8"; $env:AURA_BROWSER="brave"; python main.py`
4. **Talk or type**: say `aura hello`, or press Enter empty for mic, or just type. `stop` halts speech. Vision needs camera permission.

### Setup for Developers (Full Build)
```bash
git clone https://github.com/ShreyasP10/AURA-AGI.git
cd AURA-AGI
Copy-Item .env.example .env   # fill GROQ_AI / OPENROUTER / HUGGING_FACE_READ

# install (py313: pygame needs binary wheel, pins are relaxed in requirements.txt)
pip install --upgrade pip
pip install -r requirements.txt --only-binary pygame

# verify
python -c "import sys; sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8'); import IMPORTS; print('OK')"

# Vosk offline (optional)
# download https://github.com/alphacep/vosk-api/releases → ASSETS/Vosk/
python -c "from ENGINE.STT.vosk_recog import speech_to_text; print(list(speech_to_text('ASSETS/Vosk/vosk-model-small-en-us-0.15'))[:1])"

# push your branch
git checkout -b feature/amazing
git push -u origin feature/amazing
```

## Configuration

*Configure Keys* — AURA’s `.env` (must-have first):

| Key | Provider | Where to get | Free tier | Required? |
|-----|----------|--------------|-----------|-----------|
| `GROQ_AI` | Groq `openai/gpt-oss-20b` chat + emotion + file-finder | `console.groq.com` → `gsk_...` | **Generous free** | Recommended |
| `OPENROUTER` | OpenRouter website summarizer | `openrouter.ai/keys` → `sk-or-v1-...` (`:free` models) | $5 credit | For website summary |
| `HUGGING_FACE_READ` | HF Inference fallback chat | `huggingface.co/settings/tokens` → `hf_...` | Free | Fallback |
| `DECOHERE_AI` | `turbo.decohere.ai` | `decohere.ai` | Paid | Image only |
| `PICOVOICE_KEY` | Porcupine 4.x | `console.picovoice.ai` | Free | Or `pip install pvporcupine==1.9.5` |
| `ELEVENLABS_API_KEY` | Voice clone | `elevenlabs.io` | Limited | Optional (else edge-tts) |

**Minimal `.env`:**
```ini
GROQ_AI=gsk_...
OPENROUTER=sk-or-v1-...
HUGGING_FACE_READ=hf_...
```

**Runtime switches** (env vars, no code change):

| Var | Default | Effect |
|-----|---------|--------|
| `AURA_BROWSER` | `brave` | STT + cookie browser (`brave` with Chrome auto-fallback) |
| `AURA_HEADLESS` | `1` | `0` opens the browser window for mic debugging |
| `AURA_TEXT_MODE` | `0` | `1` keyboard-only, no browser/mic at all |
| `AURA_SILENCE_MS` | `3000` | Mic silence before turn ends (`800-8000`) |
| `AURA_FAST` | `1` | Realtime chat (early flush, 150-token cap, instant greetings) |
| `AURA_VOICE` | `1` | `0` text-only, zero TTS wait |
| `BRAVE_PATH` | auto-detect | Explicit `brave.exe` path if not found |

---

## How It Works
*How It Works* 5 steps:

1. **Choose Intent** — type or say `aura` + your wish (`generate image`, `what is in my hand`, `set dark theme`). No `aura` = casual chat. `stop` halts speech instantly.
2. **Capture** — Brave/Chrome Selenium STT (45s timeout, silence-based turn end), Vosk/whisper offline, or keyboard (`AURA_TEXT_MODE=1`).
3. **Classify** — fast local intents first (open/find/tasks/OCR/screen, zero LLM), then 3 parallel LLM checks decide *vision / image / system / website / chat*.
4. **Act** — best provider runs: LLaVA sees, Decohere draws, registry flips, or Groq chats with early sentence flush.
5. **Speak & Remember** — ordered TTS queue answers (in-process edge-tts, skips code/art), history saved to `conversation_history.json` for next turn.

---

## Data & Memory
AURA is local-first:

- **History** `TOOLS/Alpaca_DS_Converser.py` word-window `700` → `ASSETS/conversation_history.json` auto-saved on every turn.
- **Vosk models** `ASSETS/Vosk/` (large, gitignored).
- **Stream audios** `ASSETS/STREAM_AUDIOS/*.mp3` cleaned via `TOOLS/stream_audio_cleanup.py`.
- No cloud DB required — cloud only if you choose cloud LLMs.

---

## Troubleshooting — We’ve Been There

| You see | Why | Fix |
|---------|-----|-----|
| `UnicodeEncodeError charmap` @ `DevsDoCode.py` ❤️ | Windows cp1252 | `$env:PYTHONIOENCODING="utf-8"` |
| `getaddrinfo failed` @ `Hugging_Face_TEXT.py` | offline/no token | check Wi-Fi + `HUGGING_FACE_READ` |
| `pvporcupine missing access_key` | v4 breaking change | `pip install pvporcupine==1.9.5` or `PICOVOICE_KEY` |
| `pygame` build fail py313 | `distutils` removed, pinned `==2.5.2` | `pip install -r requirements.txt --only-binary pygame` (pins now `>=`) |
| `SessionNotCreatedException` / Brave window closes | Brave vs chromedriver mismatch or profile lock | `pip install -U selenium` + update Brave, or `AURA_BROWSER=chrome`, or `AURA_TEXT_MODE=1` |
| `NoSuchWindow: target window already closed` | browser already running with locked profile | close all Brave/Chrome windows; AURA uses isolated `%TEMP%\aura-*-profile` |
| `edge_tts NoAudioReceived` / missing mp3 | markdown/code spoken + concurrent throttle | auto-sanitized + serialized in-process synth; `AURA_VOICE=0` for text-only |
| `Listening...` hangs forever | Brave Shields blocks Google Speech / no mic | headed debug `AURA_HEADLESS=0`, allow mic, or Vosk offline / `AURA_TEXT_MODE=1` |
| Slow STT | browser roundtrip | switch `IMPORTS.py` to `vosk_recog`, or lower `AURA_SILENCE_MS` |

---

## What's New (v1.1)

- **TTS Concurrency Engine:** Fixed overlapping and repeating audio bugs in `edge_tts`. Sentences are now correctly queued with unique file IDs and a global playback lock, ensuring smooth, uninterrupted streaming voice.
- **Intent Classifier Overhaul:** Rewritten the core intent classifiers (`BISECTORS`) to use the lightning-fast Groq API (`llama-3.1-8b-instant`), bypassing DeepInfra's new web security locks. This fully restores **System Controls** (theme/taskbar) and **Image Generation**.
- **AURA Persona Upgrade:** Replaced the amnesiac/confused persona with a highly intelligent, helpful AI assistant (`human_response_v3_AVA`). AURA will now properly explain complex topics like CNNs instead of refusing.
- **Syntax & Stability:** Formatted the entire codebase using `ruff` and safely patched over 1,500 lines of bare `except:` blocks for flawless runtime stability.

---

## Roadmap — What’s Next (We’d love your ideas!)

- [x] **Your own wakeword** — `aura*.ppn` auto-detect + `custom_wakeword.md` guide (jarvis builtin fallback)
- [x] **Whisper offline** — `whisper_offline.py` (`faster-whisper`) fallback path
- [x] **Rich terminal UI** — opencode-style panels, hybrid voice+type, `/voice` + `/quit`
- [ ] **One-click installer** — auto-updates

> Got an idea? Open an issue — we build with the community, not just for it.

################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/Dockerfile

FROM python:3.12-slim
ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 \
    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 \
    PIP_NO_CACHE_DIR=1 \
    DATA_DIR=/data
WORKDIR /app
COPY requirements.txt /tmp/requirements.txt
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r /tmp/requirements.txt \
    && addgroup --system app \
    && adduser --system --ingroup app --home /app app \
    && mkdir -p /data \
    && chown -R app:app /app /data
COPY --chown=app:app app /app/app
USER app
EXPOSE 8080
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=5s --start-period=20s --retries=3 \
  CMD python -c "import urllib.request; urllib.request.urlopen('http://127.0.0.1:8080/health', timeout=3)"
CMD ["uvicorn", "app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8080", "--proxy-headers"]

################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/app/config.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    app_name: str
    data_dir: Path
    database_path: Path
    ollama_base_url: str
    ollama_model: str
    ollama_vision_model: str
    searxng_base_url: str
    search_result_limit: int
    request_timeout_seconds: float
    agent_step_limit: int
    max_context_chars: int
    public_origin: str
    owner_profile_name: str
    action_webhook_url: str
    action_webhook_token: str
    google_drive_sync_dir: Path | None


@lru_cache
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    data_dir = Path(os.getenv("DATA_DIR", "/data"))
    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return Settings(
        app_name=os.getenv("APP_NAME", "AURA Kiosk Agent"),
        data_dir=data_dir,
        database_path=data_dir / os.getenv("DATABASE_FILE", "agent.db"),
        ollama_base_url=os.getenv("OLLAMA_BASE_URL", "http://host.docker.internal:11434").rstrip("/"),
        ollama_model=os.getenv("OLLAMA_MODEL", "llama3.2:3b"),
        ollama_vision_model=os.getenv("OLLAMA_VISION_MODEL", ""),
        searxng_base_url=os.getenv("SEARXNG_BASE_URL", "http://searxng:8080").rstrip("/"),
        search_result_limit=max(1, min(int(os.getenv("SEARCH_RESULT_LIMIT", "6")), 12)),
        request_timeout_seconds=max(5.0, float(os.getenv("REQUEST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS", "45"))),
        agent_step_limit=max(1, min(int(os.getenv("AGENT_STEP_LIMIT", "4")), 8)),
        max_context_chars=max(2000, min(int(os.getenv("MAX_CONTEXT_CHARS", "12000")), 40000)),
        public_origin=os.getenv("PUBLIC_ORIGIN", ""),
        owner_profile_name=os.getenv("OWNER_PROFILE_NAME", "Owner"),
        action_webhook_url=os.getenv("ACTION_WEBHOOK_URL", "").rstrip("/"),
        action_webhook_token=os.getenv("ACTION_WEBHOOK_TOKEN", ""),
        google_drive_sync_dir=Path(os.environ["GOOGLE_DRIVE_SYNC_DIR"]) if os.getenv("GOOGLE_DRIVE_SYNC_DIR") else None,
    )


################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/app/db.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
import uuid
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterator


def utc_now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()


class Database:
    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
        self.path = path

    @contextmanager
    def connect(self) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:
        connection = sqlite3.connect(self.path)
        connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        connection.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
        connection.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
        try:
            yield connection
            connection.commit()
        finally:
            connection.close()

    def initialize(self, owner_name: str) -> None:
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.executescript(
                """
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profiles (
                    profile_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    display_name TEXT NOT NULL,
                    consented INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                    preferences_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS conversations (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    profile_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES profiles(profile_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                    role TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(role IN ('user', 'assistant', 'system')),
                    content TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_conversations_profile_created
                    ON conversations(profile_id, created_at DESC);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memories (
                    memory_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    profile_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES profiles(profile_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                    category TEXT NOT NULL,
                    content TEXT NOT NULL,
                    importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 2,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    last_accessed_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memories_profile_importance
                    ON memories(profile_id, importance DESC, created_at DESC);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS action_rules (
                    rule_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    profile_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES profiles(profile_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                    action_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                    display_name TEXT NOT NULL,
                    allowed_targets_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                    expires_at TEXT,
                    enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS actions (
                    action_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    profile_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES profiles(profile_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                    action_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                    summary TEXT NOT NULL,
                    payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                    status TEXT NOT NULL,
                    requires_confirmation INTEGER NOT NULL,
                    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
                    rule_id TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    expires_at TEXT
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_actions_profile_created
                    ON actions(profile_id, created_at DESC);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_events (
                    event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    profile_id TEXT,
                    event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                    details_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS skills (
                    skill_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    display_name TEXT NOT NULL,
                    description TEXT NOT NULL,
                    category TEXT NOT NULL,
                    enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                    requires_configuration INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                """
            )
            db.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO profiles(profile_id, display_name, consented, preferences_json, created_at)
                VALUES ('owner', ?, 1, '{}', ?)
                ON CONFLICT(profile_id) DO NOTHING
                """,
                (owner_name, utc_now()),
            )

    def create_profile(self, profile_id: str, display_name: str, consented: bool, preferences: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        created_at = utc_now()
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO profiles(profile_id, display_name, consented, preferences_json, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (profile_id, display_name, int(consented), json.dumps(preferences), created_at),
            )
        return self.get_profile(profile_id) or {}

    def get_profile(self, profile_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        with self.connect() as db:
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM profiles WHERE profile_id = ?", (profile_id,)).fetchone()
        return self._profile_dict(row) if row else None

    def list_profiles(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        with self.connect() as db:
            rows = db.execute("SELECT * FROM profiles ORDER BY created_at ASC").fetchall()
        return [self._profile_dict(row) for row in rows]

    def save_turn(self, profile_id: str, role: str, content: str) -> None:
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO conversations(profile_id, role, content, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (profile_id, role, content, utc_now()),
            )

    def recent_turns(self, profile_id: str, limit: int = 12) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        with self.connect() as db:
            rows = db.execute(
                "SELECT role, content, created_at FROM conversations WHERE profile_id = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?",
                (profile_id, limit),
            ).fetchall()
        return [dict(row) for row in reversed(rows)]

    def create_memory(self, profile_id: str, content: str, category: str, importance: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        memory_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        now = utc_now()
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO memories(memory_id, profile_id, category, content, importance, created_at, last_accessed_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (memory_id, profile_id, category, content, importance, now, now),
            )
        return {"memory_id": memory_id, "profile_id": profile_id, "category": category, "content": content, "importance": importance, "created_at": now}

    def recall_memories(self, profile_id: str, query: str, limit: int = 6) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        terms = [term.strip() for term in query.lower().split() if len(term.strip()) > 2][:8]
        where = " OR ".join("lower(content) LIKE ?" for _ in terms)
        parameters: list[Any] = [profile_id]
        sql = "SELECT * FROM memories WHERE profile_id = ?"
        if where:
            sql += f" AND ({where})"
            parameters.extend(f"%{term}%" for term in terms)
        sql += " ORDER BY importance DESC, last_accessed_at DESC LIMIT ?"
        parameters.append(limit)
        with self.connect() as db:
            rows = db.execute(sql, parameters).fetchall()
            if rows:
                now = utc_now()
                db.executemany(
                    "UPDATE memories SET last_accessed_at = ? WHERE memory_id = ?",
                    [(now, row["memory_id"]) for row in rows],
                )
        return [dict(row) for row in rows]

    def create_action_rule(self, rule_id: str, profile_id: str, action_type: str, display_name: str, allowed_targets: list[str], expires_at: str | None, enabled: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
        created_at = utc_now()
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.execute(
                """INSERT INTO action_rules(rule_id, profile_id, action_type, display_name, allowed_targets_json, expires_at, enabled, created_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(rule_id) DO UPDATE SET display_name = excluded.display_name, allowed_targets_json = excluded.allowed_targets_json, expires_at = excluded.expires_at, enabled = excluded.enabled""",
                (rule_id, profile_id, action_type, display_name, json.dumps(allowed_targets), expires_at, int(enabled), created_at),
            )
        return self.get_action_rule(rule_id) or {}

    def get_action_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        with self.connect() as db:
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM action_rules WHERE rule_id = ?", (rule_id,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return None
        record = dict(row)
        record["allowed_targets"] = json.loads(record.pop("allowed_targets_json"))
        record["enabled"] = bool(record["enabled"])
        return record

    def create_action(self, profile_id: str, action_type: str, summary: str, payload: dict[str, Any], status: str, requires_confirmation: bool, reason: str, rule_id: str | None = None, expires_at: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        action_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        created_at = utc_now()
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO actions(action_id, profile_id, action_type, summary, payload_json, status, requires_confirmation, reason, rule_id, created_at, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (action_id, profile_id, action_type, summary, json.dumps(payload), status, int(requires_confirmation), reason, rule_id, created_at, expires_at),
            )
        return self.get_action(action_id) or {}

    def get_action(self, action_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        with self.connect() as db:
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM actions WHERE action_id = ?", (action_id,)).fetchone()
        return self._action_dict(row) if row else None

    def set_action_status(self, action_id: str, status: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.execute("UPDATE actions SET status = ? WHERE action_id = ?", (status, action_id))
        return self.get_action(action_id)

    def audit(self, event_type: str, details: dict[str, Any], profile_id: str | None = None) -> None:
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO audit_events(event_id, profile_id, event_type, details_json, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (uuid.uuid4().hex, profile_id, event_type, json.dumps(details), utc_now()),
            )

    def seed_skills(self, skills: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
        now = utc_now()
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.executemany(
                """INSERT INTO skills(skill_id, display_name, description, category, enabled, requires_configuration, updated_at)
                VALUES (:skill_id, :display_name, :description, :category, :enabled, :requires_configuration, :updated_at)
                ON CONFLICT(skill_id) DO UPDATE SET display_name = excluded.display_name, description = excluded.description, category = excluded.category, requires_configuration = excluded.requires_configuration""",
                [{**skill, "enabled": int(skill.get("enabled", False)), "requires_configuration": int(skill.get("requires_configuration", False)), "updated_at": now} for skill in skills],
            )

    def list_skills(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        with self.connect() as db:
            rows = db.execute("SELECT * FROM skills ORDER BY category, display_name").fetchall()
        records = [dict(row) for row in rows]
        for record in records:
            record["enabled"] = bool(record["enabled"])
            record["requires_configuration"] = bool(record["requires_configuration"])
        return records

    def set_skill_enabled(self, skill_id: str, enabled: bool) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.execute("UPDATE skills SET enabled = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE skill_id = ?", (int(enabled), utc_now(), skill_id))
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM skills WHERE skill_id = ?", (skill_id,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return None
        record = dict(row)
        record["enabled"] = bool(record["enabled"])
        record["requires_configuration"] = bool(record["requires_configuration"])
        return record

    @staticmethod
    def _profile_dict(row: sqlite3.Row) -> dict[str, Any]:
        record = dict(row)
        record["consented"] = bool(record["consented"])
        record["preferences"] = json.loads(record.pop("preferences_json"))
        return record

    @staticmethod
    def _action_dict(row: sqlite3.Row) -> dict[str, Any]:
        record = dict(row)
        record["payload"] = json.loads(record.pop("payload_json"))
        record["requires_confirmation"] = bool(record["requires_confirmation"])
        return record

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import AsyncIterator

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, StreamingResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from app.adapters.ollama import OllamaClient
from app.adapters.searxng import SearxngClient
from app.config import get_settings
from app.db import Database
from app.models import (
    ActionConfirmation,
    ActionProposalRequest,
    ActionResponse,
    ActionRuleCreate,
    ChatRequest,
    DriveImportRequest,
    EnrollmentImageRequest,
    MediaCaptureRequest,
    MemoryCreate,
    ProfileCreate,
    ProfileResponse,
    SkillStateUpdate,
    TaskPlanRequest,
    VaultSaveRequest,
)
from app.services.actions import ActionPolicyService
from app.services.agent import AgentService
from app.services.executor import ActionExecutor
from app.services.google_drive import GoogleDriveMirror
from app.services.memory import MemoryService
from app.services.skills import SkillRegistry
from app.services.tasks import TaskPlanner
from app.services.vault import ProfileVault

settings = get_settings()
static_dir = Path(__file__).parent / "static"


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    database = Database(settings.database_path)
    database.initialize(settings.owner_profile_name)
    memory = MemoryService(database, settings.max_context_chars)
    app.state.database = database
    app.state.ollama = OllamaClient(settings)
    app.state.searxng = SearxngClient(settings)
    app.state.memory = memory
    app.state.agent = AgentService(settings, database, app.state.ollama, app.state.searxng, memory)
    app.state.actions = ActionPolicyService(database)
    app.state.executor = ActionExecutor(settings, database)
    app.state.tasks = TaskPlanner(database, app.state.ollama, app.state.actions)
    app.state.skills = SkillRegistry(database)
    app.state.skills.initialize()
    app.state.vault = ProfileVault(settings)
    app.state.google_drive = GoogleDriveMirror(settings, database, app.state.vault)
    yield


app = FastAPI(title=settings.app_name, version="0.1.0", lifespan=lifespan)

if settings.public_origin:
    app.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=[item.strip() for item in settings.public_origin.split(",") if item.strip()],
        allow_credentials=False,
        allow_methods=["GET", "POST"],
        allow_headers=["Content-Type"],
    )

app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=static_dir), name="static")


def database(request: Request) -> Database:
    return request.app.state.database


def actions(request: Request) -> ActionPolicyService:
    return request.app.state.actions


@app.get("/health")
async def health(request: Request) -> dict:
    ollama = await request.app.state.ollama.health()
    search = await request.app.state.searxng.health()
    return {"status": "ok" if ollama.get("reachable") else "degraded", "service": settings.app_name, "ollama": ollama, "search": search, "database": str(settings.database_path)}


@app.get("/healthz", include_in_schema=False)
async def healthz(request: Request) -> dict:
    return await health(request)


@app.get("/favicon.ico", include_in_schema=False)
async def favicon() -> FileResponse:
    # inline SVG favicon to avoid 404 noise; no static asset needed
    import tempfile

    svg = b'<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><circle cx="16" cy="16" r="14" fill="#07111f" stroke="#48c6ff" stroke-width="2"/><text x="16" y="22" text-anchor="middle" fill="#48c6ff" font-size="16" font-family="sans-serif">J</text></svg>'
    tmp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(delete=False, suffix=".svg")
    tmp.write(svg)
    tmp.close()
    return FileResponse(tmp.name, media_type="image/svg+xml")

@app.post("/api/chat/stream")
async def chat_stream(payload: ChatRequest, request: Request) -> StreamingResponse:
    async def events() -> AsyncIterator[str]:
        async for event in request.app.state.agent.stream_chat(payload.profile_id, payload.message, payload.include_web):
            yield json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
    return StreamingResponse(events(), media_type="application/x-ndjson", headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache", "X-Accel-Buffering": "no"})

@app.post("/api/profiles/{profile_id}/enrollment-image", status_code=201)
def save_enrollment_image(profile_id: str, payload: EnrollmentImageRequest, request: Request) -> dict:
    profile = database(request).get_profile(profile_id)
    if not profile:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Profile not found.")
    if not profile["consented"] or not payload.consented:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="A consenting profile and explicit capture consent are required.")
    try:
        record = request.app.state.vault.save_enrollment_image(profile_id, payload.image_data_url, payload.label)
    except ValueError as error:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(error)) from error
    database(request).audit("profile_enrollment_image_saved", record, profile_id)
    return {**record, "message": "Enrollment image stored locally for this consenting profile. No recognition or background matching was performed."}


@app.post("/api/media/capture", status_code=202)
def record_media_capture(payload: MediaCaptureRequest, request: Request) -> dict:
    if not payload.consented:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Explicit browser consent is required before media input is recorded.")
    if not database(request).get_profile(payload.profile_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Profile not found.")
    database(request).audit("media_capture_authorized", {"kind": payload.kind, "label": payload.label}, payload.profile_id)
    return {"accepted": True, "message": "Media permission state was recorded. Raw media upload is disabled until a dedicated opt-in adapter is configured."}


@app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
def kiosk() -> FileResponse:
    return FileResponse(static_dir / "index.html")

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    profile_id: str = Field(default="owner", min_length=1, max_length=96)
    message: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=6000)
    include_web: bool = True
    speak: bool = False


class ProfileCreate(BaseModel):
    profile_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=96, pattern=r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$")
    display_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=96)
    consented: bool = False
    preferences: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)


class ProfileResponse(BaseModel):
    profile_id: str
    display_name: str
    consented: bool
    preferences: dict[str, Any]
    created_at: datetime


class ActionProposalRequest(BaseModel):
    profile_id: str = Field(default="owner", min_length=1, max_length=96)
    action_type: Literal["message", "booking", "file_transfer", "location_share", "device_control"]
    summary: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=800)
    payload: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    rule_id: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=96)


class ActionConfirmation(BaseModel):
    confirmation_phrase: str = Field(min_length=2, max_length=160)


class ActionResponse(BaseModel):
    action_id: str
    action_type: str
    summary: str
    status: str
    requires_confirmation: bool
    reason: str
    expires_at: datetime | None = None


class ActionRuleCreate(BaseModel):
    rule_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=96, pattern=r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$")
    profile_id: str = Field(default="owner", min_length=1, max_length=96)
    action_type: Literal["file_transfer", "location_share", "device_control"]
    display_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    allowed_targets: list[str] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=32)
    expires_at: datetime | None = None
    enabled: bool = True


class SkillStateUpdate(BaseModel):
    enabled: bool


class DriveImportRequest(BaseModel):
    profile_id: str = Field(default="owner", min_length=1, max_length=96)
    relative_path: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=512)
    remember_excerpt: bool = False


class TaskPlanRequest(BaseModel):
    profile_id: str = Field(default="owner", min_length=1, max_length=96)
    request: str = Field(min_length=3, max_length=4000)


class VaultSaveRequest(BaseModel):
    profile_id: str = Field(default="owner", min_length=1, max_length=96)
    title: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    content: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=12000)
    category: str = Field(default="saved", min_length=1, max_length=64)


class EnrollmentImageRequest(BaseModel):
    image_data_url: str = Field(min_length=32, max_length=2_800_000)
    consented: bool
    label: str = Field(default="Galaxy Book explicit profile enrollment", max_length=160)


class MediaCaptureRequest(BaseModel):
    profile_id: str = Field(default="owner", min_length=1, max_length=96)
    kind: Literal["camera", "microphone"]
    consented: bool
    label: str = Field(default="", max_length=160)


class MemoryCreate(BaseModel):
    profile_id: str = Field(default="owner", min_length=1, max_length=96)
    content: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=4000)
    category: str = Field(default="note", min_length=1, max_length=64)
    importance: int = Field(default=2, ge=1, le=5)

################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/app/adapters/ollama.py

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from typing import Any

import httpx

from app.config import Settings


class OllamaClient:
    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
        self.base_url = settings.ollama_base_url
        self.model = settings.ollama_model
        self.timeout = settings.request_timeout_seconds

    async def health(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=min(self.timeout, 8.0)) as client:
                response = await client.get(f"{self.base_url}/api/tags")
                response.raise_for_status()
                models = [item.get("name", "") for item in response.json().get("models", [])]
            return {"reachable": True, "configured_model": self.model, "model_available": self.model in models, "models": models}
        except httpx.HTTPError as error:
            return {"reachable": False, "configured_model": self.model, "model_available": False, "error": str(error)}

    async def complete(self, messages: list[dict[str, str]], temperature: float = 0.2) -> str:
        text = ""
        async for token in self.stream(messages, temperature=temperature):
            text += token
        return text.strip()

    async def stream(self, messages: list[dict[str, str]], temperature: float = 0.2) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
        payload = {
            "model": self.model,
            "messages": messages,
            "stream": True,
            "options": {"temperature": temperature},
        }
        timeout = httpx.Timeout(self.timeout, connect=min(self.timeout, 10.0))
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=timeout) as client:
            async with client.stream("POST", f"{self.base_url}/api/chat", json=payload) as response:
                response.raise_for_status()
                async for line in response.aiter_lines():
                    if not line:
                        continue
                    chunk = json.loads(line)
                    if chunk.get("error"):
                        raise RuntimeError(str(chunk["error"]))
                    content = chunk.get("message", {}).get("content", "")
                    if content:
                        yield content
                    if chunk.get("done"):
                        return


################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/app/adapters/searxng.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

import httpx

from app.config import Settings


class SearxngClient:
    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
        self.base_url = settings.searxng_base_url
        self.limit = settings.search_result_limit
        self.timeout = settings.request_timeout_seconds

    async def health(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=min(self.timeout, 8.0)) as client:
                response = await client.get(f"{self.base_url}/healthz")
                if response.status_code not in {200, 204}:
                    response = await client.get(f"{self.base_url}/")
                response.raise_for_status()
            return {"reachable": True}
        except httpx.HTTPError as error:
            return {"reachable": False, "error": str(error)}

    async def search(self, query: str) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        params = {"q": query, "format": "json", "language": "en", "safesearch": 1}
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=self.timeout, follow_redirects=True) as client:
            response = await client.get(f"{self.base_url}/search", params=params)
            response.raise_for_status()
            payload = response.json()
        results: list[dict[str, str]] = []
        seen_urls: set[str] = set()
        for raw in payload.get("results", []):
            url = str(raw.get("url", "")).strip()
            title = str(raw.get("title", "")).strip()
            content = str(raw.get("content", "")).strip()
            if not url or url in seen_urls:
                continue
            seen_urls.add(url)
            results.append({"title": title[:300], "url": url[:2048], "content": content[:1500]})
            if len(results) >= self.limit:
                break
        return results

################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/app/services/actions.py
