from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any

from app.db import Database


class ActionPolicyService:
    CONFIRMED_ACTIONS = {"message", "booking"}
    PREAUTHORIZED_ACTIONS = {"file_transfer", "location_share", "device_control"}

    def __init__(self, database: Database) -> None:
        self.database = database

    def create_rule(self, rule_id: str, profile_id: str, action_type: str, display_name: str, allowed_targets: list[str], expires_at: datetime | None, enabled: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if action_type not in self.PREAUTHORIZED_ACTIONS:
            raise ValueError("Only file transfers, location shares, and device controls may use pre-authorized rules.")
        targets = sorted({target.strip() for target in allowed_targets if target.strip()})
        if not targets:
            raise ValueError("At least one named target is required.")
        rule = self.database.create_action_rule(rule_id, profile_id, action_type, display_name, targets, expires_at.astimezone(UTC).isoformat() if expires_at else None, enabled)
        self.database.audit("action_rule_saved", {"rule_id": rule_id, "action_type": action_type, "targets": targets}, profile_id)
        return rule

    def propose(self, profile_id: str, action_type: str, summary: str, payload: dict[str, Any], rule_id: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not self.database.get_profile(profile_id):
            raise ValueError("The selected profile does not exist.")
        if action_type in self.CONFIRMED_ACTIONS:
            expires_at = (datetime.now(UTC) + timedelta(minutes=10)).isoformat()
            action = self.database.create_action(profile_id, action_type, summary, payload, "awaiting_confirmation", True, "This action always requires the owner to speak or tap confirmation.", expires_at=expires_at)
        elif action_type in self.PREAUTHORIZED_ACTIONS:
            action = self._apply_rule(profile_id, action_type, summary, payload, rule_id)
        else:
            raise ValueError("Unsupported action type.")
        self.database.audit("action_proposed", {"action_id": action["action_id"], "type": action_type, "status": action["status"]}, profile_id)
        return action

    def confirm(self, action_id: str, confirmation_phrase: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        action = self.database.get_action(action_id)
        if not action:
            raise ValueError("The action does not exist.")
        if action["status"] != "awaiting_confirmation" or not action["requires_confirmation"]:
            raise ValueError("This action cannot be confirmed in its current state.")
        if not self._contains_confirmation(confirmation_phrase):
            raise ValueError("Confirmation phrase was not recognized. Say or type 'confirm'.")
        expires_at = action.get("expires_at")
        if expires_at and datetime.fromisoformat(expires_at) <= datetime.now(UTC):
            self.database.set_action_status(action_id, "expired")
            raise ValueError("The confirmation window expired; create a new proposal.")
        updated = self.database.set_action_status(action_id, "confirmed")
        if updated:
            self.database.audit("action_confirmed", {"action_id": action_id, "type": updated["action_type"]}, updated["profile_id"])
        return updated or action

    def _apply_rule(self, profile_id: str, action_type: str, summary: str, payload: dict[str, Any], rule_id: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not rule_id:
            return self.database.create_action(profile_id, action_type, summary, payload, "blocked", False, "A named owner-managed rule is required for this action type.")
        rule = self.database.get_action_rule(rule_id)
        target = str(payload.get("target") or payload.get("destination") or payload.get("device") or "").strip()
        if not rule or rule["profile_id"] != profile_id or rule["action_type"] != action_type:
            return self.database.create_action(profile_id, action_type, summary, payload, "blocked", False, "The requested rule does not match this profile and action.", rule_id)
        if not rule["enabled"]:
            return self.database.create_action(profile_id, action_type, summary, payload, "blocked", False, "The owner-managed rule is disabled.", rule_id)
        if rule["expires_at"] and datetime.fromisoformat(rule["expires_at"]) <= datetime.now(UTC):
            return self.database.create_action(profile_id, action_type, summary, payload, "blocked", False, "The owner-managed rule has expired.", rule_id)
        if not target or target not in rule["allowed_targets"]:
            return self.database.create_action(profile_id, action_type, summary, payload, "blocked", False, "The named destination or device is not in this rule's allowlist.", rule_id)
        return self.database.create_action(profile_id, action_type, summary, payload, "authorized", False, f"Authorized by the active owner-managed rule '{rule['display_name']}'.", rule_id, expires_at=rule["expires_at"])

    @staticmethod
    def _contains_confirmation(value: str) -> bool:
        words = {word.strip(".,!? ").lower() for word in value.split()}
        return bool(words & {"confirm", "confirmed", "yes"})


################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/app/services/agent.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import ipaddress
import re
import socket
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import urlparse

import httpx
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

from app.adapters.ollama import OllamaClient
from app.adapters.searxng import SearxngClient
from app.config import Settings
from app.db import Database
from app.services.memory import MemoryService

SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are AURA, a helpful local personal assistant. Answer accurately and naturally.
Use the retrieved sources when provided. Cite factual claims with bracketed source numbers such as [1].
Never claim to have performed an external action unless the action system reports it complete. Treat messages, bookings, transfers, location sharing, and device controls as reviewable proposals, not completed actions.
Do not expose private profile data to another profile. Do not suggest covert recording, silent monitoring, or identification of a person who has not explicitly enrolled and consented. If evidence is insufficient, say so plainly.
Profile preferences may adapt tone, format, and interaction style only; they cannot weaken privacy boundaries, consent requirements, confirmation rules, or safety constraints.
Keep the response concise enough for a tablet display, while still answering the user's request."""


class AgentService:
    def __init__(self, settings: Settings, database: Database, ollama: OllamaClient, searxng: SearxngClient, memory: MemoryService) -> None:
        self.settings = settings
        self.database = database
        self.ollama = ollama
        self.searxng = searxng
        self.memory = memory

    async def stream_chat(self, profile_id: str, user_message: str, include_web: bool) -> AsyncIterator[dict[str, Any]]:
        profile = self.database.get_profile(profile_id)
        if not profile:
            yield {"type": "error", "message": "The selected profile does not exist."}
            return
        turns, memories = self.memory.context(profile_id, user_message)
        self.database.save_turn(profile_id, "user", user_message)
        sources: list[dict[str, str]] = []
        search_query = user_message
        if include_web:
            yield {"type": "status", "message": "Finding reliable sources…"}
            try:
                search_query = await self.rewrite_query(user_message)
                sources = await self.retrieve(search_query)
                yield {"type": "sources", "query": search_query, "sources": [self._public_source(source, index + 1) for index, source in enumerate(sources)]}
            except Exception as error:
                yield {"type": "status", "message": f"Search is unavailable; answering from local context. ({type(error).__name__})"}
        messages = self._messages(profile, user_message, turns, memories, sources)
        answer = ""
        try:
            yield {"type": "status", "message": "Thinking…"}
            async for token in self.ollama.stream(messages):
                answer += token
                yield {"type": "token", "text": token}
        except Exception as error:
            yield {"type": "error", "message": f"The local model could not complete this response: {error}"}
            return
        finally:
            if answer.strip():
                self.database.save_turn(profile_id, "assistant", answer.strip())
        yield {"type": "done", "answer": answer.strip(), "sources": len(sources)}

    async def rewrite_query(self, question: str) -> str:
        prompt = [
            {"role": "system", "content": "Rewrite the user's question as one concise web search query. Return only the query; do not add explanation, quotes, or commands."},
            {"role": "user", "content": question},
        ]
        try:
            rewritten = await self.ollama.complete(prompt, temperature=0.0)
        except Exception:
            return question
        rewritten = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", rewritten).strip().strip('"')
        return rewritten[:400] if rewritten else question

    async def retrieve(self, search_query: str) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        results = await self.searxng.search(search_query)
        enriched = await asyncio.gather(*(self._enrich_result(result) for result in results), return_exceptions=True)
        sources: list[dict[str, str]] = []
        for result, extra in zip(results, enriched, strict=True):
            source = dict(result)
            if isinstance(extra, str) and extra:
                source["extracted"] = extra
            sources.append(source)
        return sources

    async def _enrich_result(self, result: dict[str, str]) -> str:
        url = result.get("url", "")
        if not self._is_public_http_url(url):
            return ""
        try:
            timeout = httpx.Timeout(10.0, connect=5.0)
            headers = {"User-Agent": "AuraKioskAgent/0.1 (+local retrieval)"}
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=timeout, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers) as client:
                response = await client.get(url)
                response.raise_for_status()
                content_type = response.headers.get("content-type", "").lower()
                if "text/html" not in content_type:
                    return ""
                html = response.text[:500_000]
            soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
            for node in soup(["script", "style", "noscript", "nav", "footer", "header", "aside"]):
                node.decompose()
            root = soup.find("article") or soup.find("main") or soup.body or soup
            text = " ".join(root.stripped_strings)
            return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)[:3500]
        except (httpx.HTTPError, ValueError):
            return ""

    def _messages(self, profile: dict[str, Any], question: str, turns: list[dict[str, str]], memories: list[dict[str, Any]], sources: list[dict[str, str]]) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        preference_note = ""
        if profile.get("preferences"):
            preference_note = f"\nProfile preferences: {profile['preferences']}"
        memory_text = "\n".join(f"- ({memory['category']}, importance {memory['importance']}) {memory['content']}" for memory in memories) or "No relevant stored memories."
        source_text = "\n\n".join("[{}] {}\nURL: {}\nSnippet: {}\nExtract: {}".format(index, source.get("title", "Untitled source"), source.get("url", ""), source.get("content", ""), source.get("extracted", "")) for index, source in enumerate(sources, start=1)) or "No web sources were retrieved."
        context = f"Active profile: {profile['display_name']} ({profile['profile_id']}).{preference_note}\n\nRelevant long-term memory:\n{memory_text}\n\nRetrieved web evidence:\n{source_text}"
        return [{"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT + "\n\n" + context}, *turns, {"role": "user", "content": question}]

    @staticmethod
    def _public_source(source: dict[str, str], number: int) -> dict[str, str | int]:
        return {"number": number, "title": source.get("title", "Untitled source"), "url": source.get("url", ""), "snippet": source.get("content", "")}

    @staticmethod
    def _is_public_http_url(value: str) -> bool:
        parsed = urlparse(value)
        if parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https"} or not parsed.hostname:
            return False
        host = parsed.hostname.lower().rstrip(".")
        if host in {"localhost", "localhost.localdomain"} or host.endswith(".local"):
            return False
        try:
            addresses = {entry[4][0] for entry in socket.getaddrinfo(host, None, proto=socket.IPPROTO_TCP)}
            return bool(addresses) and all(ipaddress.ip_address(address).is_global for address in addresses)
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            return False


################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/app/services/executor.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any

import httpx

from app.config import Settings
from app.db import Database


class ActionExecutor:
    """Deliver approved actions to one explicit, user-owned connector endpoint."""

    RETRYABLE_STATUSES = {"confirmed", "authorized", "connector_unavailable", "execution_failed"}

    def __init__(self, settings: Settings, database: Database) -> None:
        self.settings = settings
        self.database = database

    async def execute(self, action_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        action = self.database.get_action(action_id)
        if not action:
            raise ValueError("The action does not exist.")
        if action["status"] not in self.RETRYABLE_STATUSES:
            raise ValueError("This action is not approved for execution in its current state.")
        if self._is_expired(action.get("expires_at")):
            self.database.set_action_status(action_id, "expired")
            raise ValueError("The action authorization has expired. Create a new proposal.")
        if not self.settings.action_webhook_url:
            updated = self.database.set_action_status(action_id, "connector_unavailable")
            self.database.audit("action_connector_unavailable", {"action_id": action_id, "type": action["action_type"]}, action["profile_id"])
            return {"action": updated or action, "delivery": {"status": "connector_unavailable", "message": "No action connector is configured. The approved action was not sent anywhere."}}
        outbound = {
            "action_id": action["action_id"],
            "profile_id": action["profile_id"],
            "action_type": action["action_type"],
            "summary": action["summary"],
            "payload": action["payload"],
            "authorized_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
        }
        headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Aura-Action-ID": action["action_id"]}
        if self.settings.action_webhook_token:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self.settings.action_webhook_token}"
        try:
            timeout = httpx.Timeout(self.settings.request_timeout_seconds, connect=min(10.0, self.settings.request_timeout_seconds))
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=timeout, follow_redirects=False) as client:
                response = await client.post(self.settings.action_webhook_url, json=outbound, headers=headers)
            response_preview = response.text[:500]
            if 200 <= response.status_code < 300:
                updated = self.database.set_action_status(action_id, "executed")
                self.database.audit("action_executed", {"action_id": action_id, "status_code": response.status_code}, action["profile_id"])
                return {"action": updated or action, "delivery": {"status": "executed", "http_status": response.status_code, "response_preview": response_preview}}
            updated = self.database.set_action_status(action_id, "execution_failed")
            self.database.audit("action_execution_failed", {"action_id": action_id, "status_code": response.status_code}, action["profile_id"])
            return {"action": updated or action, "delivery": {"status": "execution_failed", "http_status": response.status_code, "response_preview": response_preview}}
        except httpx.HTTPError as error:
            updated = self.database.set_action_status(action_id, "execution_failed")
            self.database.audit("action_execution_failed", {"action_id": action_id, "error": type(error).__name__}, action["profile_id"])
            return {"action": updated or action, "delivery": {"status": "execution_failed", "message": f"Connector request failed: {type(error).__name__}"}}

    @staticmethod
    def _is_expired(value: str | None) -> bool:
        return bool(value and datetime.fromisoformat(value) <= datetime.now(UTC))


################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/app/services/google_drive.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from app.config import Settings
from app.db import Database
from app.services.vault import ProfileVault


class GoogleDriveMirror:
    """Imports explicitly selected text documents from a read-only mounted Drive mirror."""

    allowed_extensions = {".txt", ".md", ".csv", ".json", ".log"}
    max_file_bytes = 2_000_000
    max_import_chars = 120_000

    def __init__(self, settings: Settings, database: Database, vault: ProfileVault) -> None:
        self.root = settings.google_drive_sync_dir
        self.database = database
        self.vault = vault

    def status(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not self.root:
            return {"configured": False, "ready": False, "message": "No Google Drive mirror directory is configured.", "file_count": 0}
        ready = self.root.is_dir()
        return {"configured": True, "ready": ready, "message": "Google Drive mirror is ready." if ready else "Configured Drive mirror directory is unavailable.", "file_count": len(self.list_files(limit=250)) if ready else 0}

    def list_files(self, limit: int = 100) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        if not self.root or not self.root.is_dir():
            return []
        root = self.root.resolve()
        records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for path in sorted(root.rglob("*"), key=lambda item: str(item).lower()):
            if not path.is_file() or path.suffix.lower() not in self.allowed_extensions:
                continue
            try:
                resolved = path.resolve()
                if not resolved.is_relative_to(root):
                    continue
                size = resolved.stat().st_size
            except OSError:
                continue
            if size > self.max_file_bytes:
                continue
            records.append({"relative_path": str(resolved.relative_to(root)), "size_bytes": size, "extension": resolved.suffix.lower()})
            if len(records) >= limit:
                break
        return records

    def import_file(self, profile_id: str, relative_path: str, remember_excerpt: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not self.database.get_profile(profile_id):
            raise ValueError("Unknown profile")
        source = self._resolve_allowed_file(relative_path)
        try:
            text = source.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        except OSError as error:
            raise ValueError(f"Could not read Drive mirror file: {error}") from error
        text = text[: self.max_import_chars]
        imported = self.vault.save_text(profile_id=profile_id, title=f"Drive import — {source.name}", content=text, category="google-drive-imports")
        memory = None
        if remember_excerpt:
            excerpt = " ".join(text.split())[:1200]
            if excerpt:
                memory = self.database.create_memory(profile_id=profile_id, content=f"User explicitly imported Drive document '{source.name}': {excerpt}", category="google-drive-import", importance=2)
        self.database.audit("google_drive_file_imported", {"relative_path": str(source.relative_to(self.root.resolve() if self.root else source.parent)), "remember_excerpt": remember_excerpt, "vault_relative_path": imported["relative_path"]}, profile_id)
        return {"imported": imported, "memory": memory, "source_file": source.name}

    def _resolve_allowed_file(self, relative_path: str) -> Path:
        if not self.root or not self.root.is_dir():
            raise ValueError("Google Drive mirror is not configured or unavailable")
        root = self.root.resolve()
        candidate = (root / relative_path).resolve()
        if not candidate.is_relative_to(root):
            raise ValueError("Requested file is outside the configured Drive mirror")
        if not candidate.is_file() or candidate.suffix.lower() not in self.allowed_extensions:
            raise ValueError("Only supported text documents in the configured Drive mirror can be imported")
        try:
            if candidate.stat().st_size > self.max_file_bytes:
                raise ValueError("Drive mirror file exceeds the import size limit")
        except OSError as error:
            raise ValueError(f"Could not inspect Drive mirror file: {error}") from error
        return candidate


################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/app/services/memory.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from app.db import Database


class MemoryService:
    def __init__(self, database: Database, max_context_chars: int) -> None:
        self.database = database
        self.max_context_chars = max_context_chars

    def context(self, profile_id: str, query: str) -> tuple[list[dict[str, str]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
        memories = self.database.recall_memories(profile_id, query)
        turns = self.database.recent_turns(profile_id)
        compact_turns: list[dict[str, str]] = []
        remaining = self.max_context_chars
        for turn in reversed(turns):
            cost = len(turn["content"]) + 32
            if cost > remaining:
                continue
            compact_turns.append({"role": turn["role"], "content": turn["content"]})
            remaining -= cost
        compact_turns.reverse()
        return compact_turns, memories

    def remember(self, profile_id: str, content: str, category: str = "note", importance: int = 2) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.database.create_memory(profile_id, content, category, importance)

        return record

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any

from app.adapters.ollama import OllamaClient
from app.db import Database
from app.services.actions import ActionPolicyService


class TaskPlanner:
    """Turn a natural-language goal into an explainable, non-executing task plan."""

    def __init__(self, database: Database, ollama: OllamaClient, actions: ActionPolicyService) -> None:
        self.database = database
        self.ollama = ollama
        self.actions = actions

    async def plan(self, profile_id: str, request: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        profile = self.database.get_profile(profile_id)
        if not profile:
            raise ValueError("The selected profile does not exist.")
        action_type = self._classify_action(request)
        plan = self._deterministic_steps(request, action_type)
        proposal: dict[str, Any] | None = None
        if action_type:
            payload = self._build_payload(request, action_type)
            proposal = self.actions.propose(profile_id=profile_id, action_type=action_type, summary=request.strip()[:240], payload=payload, rule_id=None)
        refinement = await self._optional_refinement(request, action_type)
        if refinement:
            plan[0] = refinement
        self.database.audit("task_planned", {"action_type": action_type, "has_proposal": bool(proposal), "request_preview": request.strip()[:160]}, profile_id)
        return {"profile_id": profile_id, "request": request.strip(), "steps": plan, "action_type": action_type, "proposal": proposal, "note": self._note_for(proposal)}

    async def _optional_refinement(self, request: str, action_type: str | None) -> str:
        prompt = [
            {"role": "system", "content": "Write one concise first step for a personal assistant task plan. Do not claim an action has happened. Return one sentence only."},
            {"role": "user", "content": f"Task: {request}\nAction type: {action_type or 'research'}"},
        ]
        try:
            result = await self.ollama.complete(prompt, temperature=0.1)
            result = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", result).strip()
            return result[:240] if result else "Clarify the relevant details and gather the information needed to proceed."
        except Exception:
            return "Clarify the relevant details and gather the information needed to proceed."

    @staticmethod
    def _classify_action(request: str) -> str | None:
        lowered = request.lower()
        if any(token in lowered for token in ("book ", "booking", "reservation", "reserve a", "reserve table")):
            return "booking"
        if any(token in lowered for token in ("send ", "message ", "text ", "email ")):
            return "message"
        if any(token in lowered for token in ("transfer", "copy file", "send file", "upload file")):
            return "file_transfer"
        if any(token in lowered for token in ("share location", "send my location", "location share")):
            return "location_share"
        if any(token in lowered for token in ("turn on", "turn off", "switch on", "switch off", "control device")):
            return "device_control"
        return None

    @staticmethod
    def _build_payload(request: str, action_type: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        target = ""
        target_match = re.search(r"\b(?:to|with|for|on)\s+([A-Za-z0-9 _.'-]{2,80})", request, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        if target_match:
            target = target_match.group(1).strip(" .")
        if action_type == "device_control":
            device_match = re.search(r"(?:turn|switch)\s+(?:on|off)\s+(.+)$", request, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
            target = device_match.group(1).strip(" .") if device_match else target
            return {"device": target, "request": request.strip()}
        if action_type == "file_transfer":
            return {"destination": target, "request": request.strip()}
        if action_type == "location_share":
            return {"target": target, "request": request.strip()}
        return {"target": target, "request": request.strip()}

    @staticmethod
    def _deterministic_steps(request: str, action_type: str | None) -> list[str]:
        base = [
            "Clarify the relevant details and gather the information needed to proceed.",
            "Present a concise result, draft, or availability summary on the kiosk.",
        ]
        if action_type in {"message", "booking"}:
            base.append("Ask for a spoken or on-screen confirmation immediately before the external action is sent.")
        elif action_type in {"file_transfer", "location_share", "device_control"}:
            base.append("Check for a matching active owner-managed rule and named target before any connector delivery.")
        else:
            base.append("Save only information the active profile explicitly asks to retain.")
        return base

    @staticmethod
    def _note_for(proposal: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str:
        if not proposal:
            return "This is a research or planning task; no external action has been proposed."
        if proposal["status"] == "awaiting_confirmation":
            return "A proposal was created, but it will not be delivered until you confirm it."
        if proposal["status"] == "authorized":
            return "A bounded owner-managed rule authorizes this named action; connector delivery remains separate."
        return proposal["reason"]


def summary_safe(request: str) -> str:
    return request.strip()[:240]


################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/app/services/vault.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import binascii
import re
from datetime import UTC, datetime

from app.config import Settings


class ProfileVault:
    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
        self.root = settings.data_dir / "profiles"
        self.root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def save_text(self, profile_id: str, title: str, content: str, category: str) -> dict[str, str]:
        profile_dir = self.root / self._safe_name(profile_id)
        category_dir = profile_dir / self._safe_name(category)
        category_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        timestamp = datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
        file_name = f"{timestamp}-{self._safe_name(title)[:64]}.md"
        path = category_dir / file_name
        path.write_text("# " + title.strip() + "\n\n" + f"Saved at: {datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()}\n" + f"Profile: {profile_id}\n" + f"Category: {category}\n\n" + content.strip() + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return {"profile_id": profile_id, "category": category, "title": title.strip(), "file_name": file_name, "relative_path": str(path.relative_to(self.root))}

    def save_enrollment_image(self, profile_id: str, image_data_url: str, label: str) -> dict[str, str]:
        """Persist one explicit profile-enrollment image locally without recognition processing."""
        match = re.fullmatch(r"data:image/(jpeg|png|webp);base64,([A-Za-z0-9+/=\s]+)", image_data_url)
        if not match:
            raise ValueError("Enrollment image must be a JPEG, PNG, or WebP data URL.")
        try:
            image_bytes = base64.b64decode(match.group(2), validate=True)
        except (binascii.Error, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ValueError("Enrollment image could not be decoded.") from exc
        if not image_bytes or len(image_bytes) > 2 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError("Enrollment image must be between 1 byte and 2 MB.")
        extension = {"jpeg": "jpg", "png": "png", "webp": "webp"}[match.group(1)]
        profile_dir = self.root / self._safe_name(profile_id) / "enrollment"
        profile_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        timestamp = datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
        file_name = f"{timestamp}-profile-image.{extension}"
        path = profile_dir / file_name
        path.write_bytes(image_bytes)
        return {"profile_id": profile_id, "label": label.strip() or "Galaxy Book explicit profile enrollment", "file_name": file_name, "relative_path": str(path.relative_to(self.root))}

    def list_files(self, profile_id: str, limit: int = 60) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        profile_dir = self.root / self._safe_name(profile_id)
        if not profile_dir.exists():
            return []
        files = sorted(profile_dir.rglob("*.md"), key=lambda item: item.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True)[:limit]
        return [{"relative_path": str(path.relative_to(self.root)), "category": path.parent.name, "file_name": path.name, "updated_at": datetime.fromtimestamp(path.stat().st_mtime, UTC).isoformat()} for path in files]

    @staticmethod
    def _safe_name(value: str) -> str:
        name = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]+", "-", value.strip()).strip("-").lower()
        return name or "untitled"

  activeProfile: "owner",
  speakingReplies: false,
  isBusy: false,
  lastAssistantReply: "",
  cameraStream: null,
  wakeWordEnabled: false,
  wakeRecognition: null,
  wakePhrase: "aura",
};

function setStatus(message) {
  elements.status.textContent = message;
}
function setBusy(busy) {
  state.isBusy = busy;
  elements.send.disabled = busy;
  elements.input.disabled = busy;
  elements.send.textContent = busy ? "…" : "↑";
}
async function api(path, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(path, {
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...(options.headers || {}) },
    ...options,
  });
  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") || "";
  const payload = contentType.includes("application/json") ? await response.json() : await response.text();
  if (!response.ok) {
    const detail = typeof payload === "object" ? payload.detail : payload;
    throw new Error(detail || `Request failed (${response.status})`);
  }
  return payload;
}
function scrollToEnd() {
  requestAnimationFrame(() => window.scrollTo({ top: document.body.scrollHeight, behavior: "smooth" }));
}
function createMessage(role, content = "") {
  const node = $("#messageTemplate").content.firstElementChild.cloneNode(true);
  node.classList.add(role === "user" ? "is-user" : "is-assistant");
  node.querySelector(".message-label").textContent = role === "user" ? "YOU" : "AURA";
  node.querySelector(".message-body").textContent = content;
  elements.chat.appendChild(node);
  scrollToEnd();
  return node;
}
function addSources(messageNode, sources) {
  if (!sources?.length) return;
  const wrap = document.createElement("section");
  wrap.className = "sources";
  const heading = document.createElement("h4");
  heading.textContent = "SOURCES";
  wrap.appendChild(heading);
  sources.forEach((source) => {
    const link = document.createElement("a");
    link.className = "source-card";
    link.href = source.url;
    link.target = "_blank";
    link.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
    link.textContent = `[${source.number}] ${source.title || "Untitled source"}`;
    if (source.snippet) {
      const snippet = document.createElement("span");
      snippet.textContent = source.snippet;
      link.appendChild(snippet);
    }
    wrap.appendChild(link);
  });
  messageNode.appendChild(wrap);
}
function autosize() {
  elements.input.style.height = "auto";
  elements.input.style.height = `${Math.min(elements.input.scrollHeight, 130)}px`;
}
async function submitMessage(message = elements.input.value.trim()) {
  if (!message || state.isBusy) return;
  elements.welcome.hidden = true;
  createMessage("user", message);
  elements.input.value = "";
  autosize();
  setBusy(true);
  setStatus("Connecting to AURA…");
  const assistantNode = createMessage("assistant", "");
  const body = assistantNode.querySelector(".message-body");
  let sourceData = [];
  let fullAnswer = "";
  try {
    const response = await fetch("/api/chat/stream", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        profile_id: state.activeProfile,
        message,
        include_web: elements.web.checked,
        speak: state.speakingReplies,
      }),
    });
    if (!response.ok || !response.body) {
      throw new Error(`Chat service failed (${response.status})`);
    }
    const reader = response.body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    let buffer = "";
    while (true) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
      const rows = buffer.split("\n");
      buffer = rows.pop() || "";
      for (const row of rows) {
        if (!row.trim()) continue;
        handleChatEvent(JSON.parse(row), body, assistantNode, (answer) => { fullAnswer = answer; }, (sources) => { sourceData = sources; });
      }
    }
    if (buffer.trim()) {
      handleChatEvent(JSON.parse(buffer), body, assistantNode, (answer) => { fullAnswer = answer; }, (sources) => { sourceData = sources; });
    }
    if (sourceData.length) addSources(assistantNode, sourceData);
    state.lastAssistantReply = fullAnswer || body.textContent.trim();
    if (state.speakingReplies && state.lastAssistantReply) speak(state.lastAssistantReply);
    setStatus("Ready");
  } catch (error) {
    body.textContent = `I could not complete that request: ${error.message}`;
    setStatus("Connection issue");
  } finally {
    setBusy(false);
    scrollToEnd();
  }
}
function handleChatEvent(event, body, node, setAnswer, setSources) {
  if (event.type === "status") {
    setStatus(event.message);
  } else if (event.type === "token") {
    body.textContent += event.text;
    scrollToEnd();
  } else if (event.type === "sources") {
    setSources(event.sources || []);
  } else if (event.type === "error") {
    body.textContent += `\n\n${event.message}`;
    setStatus("Request finished with an issue");
  } else if (event.type === "done") {
    setAnswer(event.answer || body.textContent);
  }
}
function speak(text) {
  if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) {
    setStatus("This browser does not provide text-to-speech.");
    return;
  }
  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text.slice(0, 7000));
  utterance.rate = 1;
  utterance.pitch = 1;
  window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
}
function updateWakeWordButton() {
  elements.wakeWord.textContent = `Wake word: ${state.wakeWordEnabled ? "On" : "Off"}`;
  elements.wakeWord.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(state.wakeWordEnabled));
}
function stopWakeWord() {
  state.wakeWordEnabled = false;
  const recognition = state.wakeRecognition;
  state.wakeRecognition = null;
  if (recognition) {
    recognition.onend = null;
    try { recognition.stop(); } catch { /* Already stopped. */ }
  }
  updateWakeWordButton();
}
function startWakeWord() {
  const Recognition = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
  if (!Recognition) {
    setStatus("Wake-word listening is not supported by this browser. Use the talk button instead.");
    return;
  }
  const recognition = new Recognition();
  recognition.lang = navigator.language || "en-AU";
  recognition.interimResults = true;
  recognition.continuous = true;
  state.wakeWordEnabled = true;
  state.wakeRecognition = recognition;
  updateWakeWordButton();
  setStatus(`Wake-word listener is visibly on. Say ${state.wakePhrase} to prepare a message; it cannot send or control anything.`);
  recognition.onresult = (event) => {
    for (let index = event.resultIndex; index < event.results.length; index += 1) {
      const transcript = event.results[index][0].transcript.trim();
      if (transcript.toLowerCase().includes(state.wakePhrase)) {
        setStatus(`${state.wakePhrase[0].toUpperCase()}${state.wakePhrase.slice(1)} heard. Tap talk or type your request; no action has been performed.`);
        break;
      }
    }
  };
  recognition.onerror = (event) => {
    if (event.error === "not-allowed" || event.error === "service-not-allowed") {
      setStatus("Wake-word microphone permission was not granted.");
      stopWakeWord();
    } else {
      setStatus(`Wake-word listener issue: ${event.error}`);
    }
  };
  recognition.onend = () => {
    if (!state.wakeWordEnabled || state.wakeRecognition !== recognition) return;
    window.setTimeout(() => {
      if (state.wakeWordEnabled && state.wakeRecognition === recognition) {
        try { recognition.start(); } catch { /* Browser may be restarting the recognizer. */ }
      }
    }, 250);
  };
  try {
    recognition.start();
  } catch (error) {
    setStatus(`Could not start wake-word listening: ${error.message}`);
    stopWakeWord();
  }
}
function toggleWakeWord() {
  if (state.wakeWordEnabled) stopWakeWord();
  else startWakeWord();
}
function startRecognition() {
  if (state.wakeWordEnabled) stopWakeWord();
  const Recognition = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
  if (!Recognition) {
    setStatus("Speech recognition is not supported by this browser. You can still type or use text-to-speech.");
    return;
  }
  const recognition = new Recognition();
  recognition.lang = navigator.language || "en-AU";
  recognition.interimResults = true;
  recognition.continuous = false;
  let finalText = "";
  setStatus("Listening… speak now.");
  $("#voiceButton").classList.add("is-listening");
  recognition.onresult = (event) => {
    let interim = "";
    for (let i = event.resultIndex; i < event.results.length; i += 1) {
      if (event.results[i].isFinal) finalText += event.results[i][0].transcript;
      else interim += event.results[i][0].transcript;
    }
    elements.input.value = `${finalText} ${interim}`.trim();
    autosize();
  };
  recognition.onerror = (event) => setStatus(`Voice input issue: ${event.error}`);
  recognition.onend = () => {
    $("#voiceButton").classList.remove("is-listening");
    if (finalText.trim()) setStatus("Voice input ready. Tap send when you are ready.");
    else if (elements.status.textContent === "Listening… speak now.") setStatus("Ready");
  };
  recognition.start();
}
async function refreshHealth() {
  try {
    const health = await api("/health");
    const ok = health.ollama?.reachable;
    elements.health.classList.toggle("is-pending", false);
    elements.health.classList.toggle("is-down", !ok);
    elements.health.title = ok ? "Local model service is reachable" : "Local model service needs attention";
    if (!ok) setStatus("Local model is not yet reachable. Check the Docker host and Ollama settings.");
  } catch {
    elements.health.classList.remove("is-pending");
    elements.health.classList.add("is-down");
    elements.health.title = "Agent server is unavailable";
  }
}
async function loadProfiles() {
  const profiles = await api("/api/profiles");
  elements.profiles.replaceChildren();
  profiles.forEach((profile) => {
    const option = document.createElement("option");
    option.value = profile.profile_id;
    option.textContent = profile.display_name;
    elements.profiles.appendChild(option);
  });
  if (!profiles.some((profile) => profile.profile_id === state.activeProfile)) state.activeProfile = profiles[0]?.profile_id || "owner";
  elements.profiles.value = state.activeProfile;
}
async function loadSkills() {
  const { skills } = await api("/api/skills");
  elements.skills.replaceChildren();
  skills.forEach((skill) => {
    const card = document.createElement("article");
    card.className = "skill";
    const top = document.createElement("div");
    top.className = "skill-top";
    const text = document.createElement("div");
    const heading = document.createElement("h3");
    heading.textContent = skill.display_name;
    const category = document.createElement("small");
    category.textContent = `${skill.category}${skill.requires_configuration ? " · setup required" : ""}`;
    text.append(heading, category);
    const switchLabel = document.createElement("label");
    switchLabel.className = "switch";
    switchLabel.title = `Enable ${skill.display_name}`;
    const input = document.createElement("input");
    input.type = "checkbox";
    input.checked = skill.enabled;
    input.addEventListener("change", async () => {
      input.disabled = true;
      try {
        await api(`/api/skills/${encodeURIComponent(skill.skill_id)}`, { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ enabled: input.checked }) });
        setStatus(`${skill.display_name}: ${input.checked ? "enabled" : "disabled"}`);
      } catch (error) {
        input.checked = !input.checked;
        setStatus(error.message);
      } finally {
        input.disabled = false;
      }
    });
    const slider = document.createElement("span");
    slider.className = "slider";
    switchLabel.append(input, slider);
    top.append(text, switchLabel);
    const description = document.createElement("p");
    description.textContent = skill.description;
    card.append(top, description);
    elements.skills.appendChild(card);
  });
}
async function loadDriveFiles() {
  try {
    const response = await api("/api/google-drive/files");
    const { status, files } = response;
    elements.driveStatus.textContent = status.message;
    elements.driveFiles.replaceChildren();
    if (!status.ready) return;
    if (!files.length) {
      const empty = document.createElement("p");
      empty.className = "drive-empty";
      empty.textContent = "No supported text documents are available in the mounted Drive folder.";
      elements.driveFiles.appendChild(empty);
      return;
    }
    files.forEach((file) => {
      const row = document.createElement("article");
      row.className = "drive-file";
      const title = document.createElement("strong");
      title.textContent = file.relative_path;
      const size = document.createElement("small");
      size.textContent = `${Math.max(1, Math.ceil(file.size_bytes / 1024))} KB`;
      const rememberLabel = document.createElement("label");
      rememberLabel.className = "drive-remember";
      const remember = document.createElement("input");
      remember.type = "checkbox";
      rememberLabel.append(remember, document.createTextNode("Add excerpt to profile memory"));
      const importButton = document.createElement("button");
      importButton.type = "button";
      importButton.className = "text-button";
      importButton.textContent = "Import to active profile";
      importButton.addEventListener("click", async () => {
        importButton.disabled = true;
        try {
          await api("/api/google-drive/import", {
            method: "POST",
            body: JSON.stringify({
              profile_id: state.activeProfile,
              relative_path: file.relative_path,
              remember_excerpt: remember.checked,
            }),
          });
          setStatus(`Imported ${file.relative_path} into this profile’s private vault.`);
        } catch (error) {
          setStatus(error.message);
        } finally {
          importButton.disabled = false;
        }
      });
      row.append(title, size, rememberLabel, importButton);
      elements.driveFiles.appendChild(row);
    });
  } catch (error) {
    elements.driveStatus.textContent = `Drive mirror unavailable: ${error.message}`;
  }
}
function openPanel() {
  elements.sidePanel.classList.add("is-open");
  elements.sidePanel.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "false");
  elements.scrim.hidden = false;
}
function closePanel() {
  elements.sidePanel.classList.remove("is-open");
  elements.sidePanel.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  elements.scrim.hidden = true;
}
async function createProfile(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  const displayName = elements.profileName.value.trim();
  const profileId = elements.profileId.value.trim();
  if (!displayName || !profileId) return;
  try {
    await api("/api/profiles", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ profile_id: profileId, display_name: displayName, consented: elements.profileConsent.checked, preferences: {} }),
    });
    state.activeProfile = profileId;
    await loadProfiles();
    elements.profileDialog.close();
    elements.profileForm.reset();
    setStatus(`Profile ${displayName} is active.`);
  } catch (error) {
    setStatus(error.message);
  }
}
async function startCamera() {
  if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
    elements.cameraHint.textContent = "This browser does not expose camera access. Use a recent Chromium or Firefox browser over HTTPS.";
    return;
  }
  try {
    state.cameraStream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: { facingMode: "user" }, audio: false });
    elements.cameraPreview.srcObject = state.cameraStream;
    elements.cameraCapture.disabled = false;
    elements.enrollCamera.disabled = !elements.enrollmentConsent.checked;
    elements.cameraHint.textContent = "Camera is on. Capture audit stores no image. Saving an enrollment photo requires the visible consent box and stores only a local profile image.";
  } catch (error) {
    elements.cameraHint.textContent = `Camera access was not granted: ${error.message}`;
  }
}
function stopCamera() {
  if (state.cameraStream) state.cameraStream.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
  state.cameraStream = null;
  elements.cameraPreview.srcObject = null;
  elements.cameraCapture.disabled = true;
  elements.enrollCamera.disabled = true;
  elements.cameraHint.textContent = "Camera is off. Start it only when you are ready.";
}
function drawCameraFrame() {
  const video = elements.cameraPreview;
  const sourceWidth = video.videoWidth || 640;
  const sourceHeight = video.videoHeight || 480;
  const scale = Math.min(1, 640 / sourceWidth, 480 / sourceHeight);
  elements.cameraCanvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(sourceWidth * scale));
  elements.cameraCanvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(sourceHeight * scale));
  elements.cameraCanvas.getContext("2d").drawImage(video, 0, 0, elements.cameraCanvas.width, elements.cameraCanvas.height);
  return elements.cameraCanvas;
}
async function captureCamera() {
  if (!state.cameraStream) return;
  drawCameraFrame();
  try {
    await api("/api/media/capture", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ profile_id: state.activeProfile, kind: "camera", consented: true, label: "Galaxy Book explicit capture" }),
    });
    elements.cameraHint.textContent = "Capture acknowledged. Image analysis/storage adapters are intentionally disabled until configured.";
  } catch (error) {
    elements.cameraHint.textContent = error.message;
  }
}
async function saveEnrollmentImage() {
  if (!state.cameraStream) {
    elements.cameraHint.textContent = "Start the camera before saving an enrollment photo.";
    return;
  }
  if (!elements.enrollmentConsent.checked) {
    elements.cameraHint.textContent = "Check the visible consent box before saving a profile enrollment photo.";
    return;
  }
  elements.enrollCamera.disabled = true;
  try {
    const imageDataUrl = drawCameraFrame().toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.82);
    const record = await api(`/api/profiles/${encodeURIComponent(state.activeProfile)}/enrollment-image`, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ image_data_url: imageDataUrl, consented: true, label: "Galaxy Book explicit profile enrollment" }),
    });
    elements.cameraHint.textContent = `Enrollment photo saved locally for ${state.activeProfile}. No background recognition or matching was performed.`;
    setStatus(`Saved ${record.file_name} to the active profile’s local enrollment folder.`);
  } catch (error) {
    elements.cameraHint.textContent = `Enrollment image was not saved: ${error.message}`;
  } finally {
    elements.enrollCamera.disabled = !elements.enrollmentConsent.checked || !state.cameraStream;
  }
}
async function planTask() {
  const request = elements.input.value.trim();
  if (!request || state.isBusy) {
    setStatus("Type or dictate a task first, then choose Plan task.");
    return;
  }
  elements.welcome.hidden = true;
  createMessage("user", `Plan task: ${request}`);
  elements.input.value = "";
  autosize();
  setBusy(true);
  setStatus("Planning a safe next step…");
  const assistantNode = createMessage("assistant", "Planning this task…");
  const body = assistantNode.querySelector(".message-body");
  try {
    const plan = await api("/api/tasks/plan", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ profile_id: state.activeProfile, request }),
    });
    body.textContent = `TASK PLAN\n\n${plan.steps.map((step, index) => `${index + 1}. ${step}`).join("\n")}`;
    if (plan.proposal) addActionControls(assistantNode, plan.proposal, plan.note);
    else body.textContent += `\n\n${plan.note}`;
    state.lastAssistantReply = body.textContent;
    setStatus("Task plan ready.");
  } catch (error) {
    body.textContent = `I could not plan that task: ${error.message}`;
    setStatus("Task plan unavailable");
  } finally {
    setBusy(false);
    scrollToEnd();
  }
}
function addActionControls(messageNode, proposal, note = "") {
  const previous = messageNode.querySelector(".action-card");
  if (previous) previous.remove();
  const card = document.createElement("section");
  card.className = "action-card";
  const title = document.createElement("h4");
  title.textContent = "EXTERNAL ACTION";
  const summary = document.createElement("p");
  summary.textContent = proposal.summary;
  const stateText = document.createElement("p");
  stateText.className = "action-state";
  stateText.textContent = note || `Status: ${proposal.status}`;
  const controls = document.createElement("div");
  controls.className = "action-controls";
  card.append(title, summary, stateText, controls);
  messageNode.appendChild(card);
  const addButton = (label, handler) => {
    const button = document.createElement("button");
    button.type = "button";
    button.className = "text-button";
    button.textContent = label;
    button.addEventListener("click", handler);
    controls.appendChild(button);
  };
  const refresh = (next, nextNote = "") => addActionControls(messageNode, next, nextNote);
  if (proposal.status === "awaiting_confirmation") {
    const typedEntry = document.createElement("div");
    typedEntry.className = "confirmation-entry";
    const phraseInput = document.createElement("input");
    phraseInput.type = "text";
    phraseInput.maxLength = 80;
    phraseInput.placeholder = "Type confirm";
    phraseInput.setAttribute("aria-label", "Type confirm to approve this action");
    const typedButton = document.createElement("button");
    typedButton.type = "button";
    typedButton.className = "text-button";
    typedButton.textContent = "Confirm typed";
    typedButton.addEventListener("click", () => confirmAction(proposal.action_id, phraseInput.value, refresh));
    typedEntry.append(phraseInput, typedButton);
    controls.appendChild(typedEntry);
    addButton("Confirm by voice", () => captureActionConfirmation(proposal.action_id, refresh));
  } else if (["confirmed", "authorized", "connector_unavailable", "execution_failed"].includes(proposal.status)) {
    addButton("Deliver to configured connector", async () => {
      try {
        const outcome = await api(`/api/actions/${encodeURIComponent(proposal.action_id)}/execute`, { method: "POST" });
        refresh(outcome.action, outcome.delivery.message || `Delivery status: ${outcome.delivery.status}`);
        setStatus(`Action delivery: ${outcome.delivery.status}`);
      } catch (error) {
        setStatus(error.message);
      }
    });
  }
}
async function confirmAction(actionId, phrase, refresh) {
  try {
    const action = await api(`/api/actions/${encodeURIComponent(actionId)}/confirm`, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ confirmation_phrase: phrase }),
    });
    refresh(action, "Confirmed. Review once more, then choose delivery to the configured connector.");
    setStatus("Action confirmed; it has not been delivered yet.");
  } catch (error) {
    setStatus(error.message);
  }
}
function captureActionConfirmation(actionId, refresh) {
  const Recognition = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
  if (!Recognition) {
    setStatus("Voice confirmation is not supported here. Use typed confirmation instead.");
    return;
  }
  const recognition = new Recognition();
  recognition.lang = navigator.language || "en-AU";
  recognition.interimResults = false;
  recognition.continuous = false;
  setStatus("Listening for confirmation. Say confirm when ready.");
  recognition.onresult = (event) => confirmAction(actionId, event.results[0][0].transcript, refresh);
  recognition.onerror = (event) => setStatus(`Voice confirmation issue: ${event.error}`);
  recognition.start();
}
async function saveLastReply() {
  if (!state.lastAssistantReply) {
    setStatus("There is no completed AURA reply to save yet.");
    return;
  }
  try {
    await api("/api/vault", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({
        profile_id: state.activeProfile,
        title: "Saved AURA response",
        content: state.lastAssistantReply,
        category: "saved-responses",
      }),
    });
    setStatus("Saved to this profile’s private content folder.");
  } catch (error) {
    setStatus(error.message);
  }
}
function bindEvents() {
  elements.form.addEventListener("submit", (event) => { event.preventDefault(); submitMessage(); });
  elements.input.addEventListener("input", autosize);
  elements.profiles.addEventListener("change", () => { state.activeProfile = elements.profiles.value; setStatus("Profile changed. Memory is now separated for this person."); });
  $("#voiceButton").addEventListener("click", startRecognition);
  $("#speakToggle").addEventListener("click", (event) => {
    state.speakingReplies = !state.speakingReplies;
    event.currentTarget.textContent = `Voice replies: ${state.speakingReplies ? "On" : "Off"}`;
    event.currentTarget.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(state.speakingReplies));
  });
  elements.wakeWord.addEventListener("click", toggleWakeWord);
  $("#saveMemoryButton").addEventListener("click", saveLastReply);
  $("#planTaskButton").addEventListener("click", planTask);
  $("#refreshDriveButton").addEventListener("click", loadDriveFiles);
  $("#menuButton").addEventListener("click", openPanel);
  $("#closePanelButton").addEventListener("click", closePanel);
  elements.scrim.addEventListener("click", closePanel);
  $("#newProfileButton").addEventListener("click", () => elements.profileDialog.showModal());
  elements.profileForm.addEventListener("submit", createProfile);
  $("#cameraButton").addEventListener("click", () => elements.cameraDialog.showModal());
  elements.cameraStart.addEventListener("click", startCamera);
  elements.cameraStop.addEventListener("click", stopCamera);
  elements.cameraCapture.addEventListener("click", captureCamera);
  elements.enrollmentConsent.addEventListener("change", () => {
    elements.enrollCamera.disabled = !elements.enrollmentConsent.checked || !state.cameraStream;
  });
  elements.enrollCamera.addEventListener("click", saveEnrollmentImage);
  elements.cameraDialog.addEventListener("close", stopCamera);
  document.querySelectorAll(".suggestion").forEach((button) => button.addEventListener("click", () => submitMessage(button.textContent)));
  window.addEventListener("beforeunload", stopWakeWord);
}
async function initialize() {
  bindEvents();
  autosize();
  try {
    await Promise.all([loadProfiles(), loadSkills(), loadDriveFiles(), refreshHealth()]);
  } catch (error) {
    setStatus(`Startup issue: ${error.message}`);
  }
  window.setInterval(refreshHealth, 30000);
}
initialize();

################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/tests/test_agent.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

from app.db import Database
from app.services.agent import AgentService
from app.services.memory import MemoryService


class FakeOllama:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.last_messages = []
        self.complete_calls = 0

    async def complete(self, messages, temperature=0.0):
        self.complete_calls += 1
        return "quiet restaurants Melbourne"

    async def stream(self, messages):
        self.last_messages = messages
        for token in ("A quiet restaurant is a good fit", " [1]."):
            yield token


class FakeSearxng:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.queries = []

    async def search(self, query):
        self.queries.append(query)
        return [{"title": "Restaurant guide", "url": "https://example.org/guide", "content": "A guide to quiet restaurants."}]


async def collect_events(service: AgentService, profile_id: str, text: str, include_web: bool):
    return [event async for event in service.stream_chat(profile_id, text, include_web)]


class AgentServiceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.database = Database(Path(self.temp_dir.name) / "agent.db")
        self.database.initialize("Owner")
        self.database.create_profile("alex", "Alex", True, {"dining": "quiet restaurants"})
        self.memory = MemoryService(self.database, 8000)
        self.memory.remember("alex", "Alex prefers low-noise venues.", "preference", 5)
        self.ollama = FakeOllama()
        self.searxng = FakeSearxng()
        self.service = AgentService(SimpleNamespace(), self.database, self.ollama, self.searxng, self.memory)
        self.service._enrich_result = AsyncMock(return_value="Extracted restaurant guide text.")

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        self.temp_dir.cleanup()

    def test_streams_answer_and_sources_for_web_request(self) -> None:
        events = asyncio.run(collect_events(self.service, "alex", "Find a low-noise dinner", True))
        types = [event["type"] for event in events]
        self.assertIn("sources", types)
        self.assertIn("token", types)
        self.assertEqual(events[-1]["type"], "done")
        source_event = next(event for event in events if event["type"] == "sources")
        self.assertEqual(source_event["sources"][0]["url"], "https://example.org/guide")
        self.assertIn("quiet restaurants Melbourne", self.searxng.queries)
        system_message = self.ollama.last_messages[0]["content"]
        self.assertIn("Alex prefers low-noise venues", system_message)
        self.assertIn("Extracted restaurant guide text", system_message)

    def test_unknown_profile_returns_error_event(self) -> None:
        events = asyncio.run(collect_events(self.service, "missing", "Hello", False))
        self.assertEqual(events, [{"type": "error", "message": "The selected profile does not exist."}])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/tests/test_core.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import tempfile
import unittest
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.db import Database
from app.services.actions import ActionPolicyService
from app.services.executor import ActionExecutor
from app.services.memory import MemoryService
from app.services.skills import SkillRegistry
from app.services.vault import ProfileVault


class AgentCoreTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.database = Database(Path(self.temp_dir.name) / "agent.db")
        self.database.initialize("Owner")
        self.actions = ActionPolicyService(self.database)

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        self.temp_dir.cleanup()

    def test_memory_is_profile_scoped(self) -> None:
        self.database.create_profile("alex", "Alex", True, {})
        memory = MemoryService(self.database, 8000)
        memory.remember("alex", "Alex prefers quiet restaurants.", "preference", 4)
        self.assertEqual(memory.context("owner", "quiet restaurants")[1], [])
        recalled = memory.context("alex", "quiet restaurants")[1]
        self.assertEqual(len(recalled), 1)

    def test_message_needs_confirmation(self) -> None:
        action = self.actions.propose("owner", "message", "Send a dinner message", {"target": "Ava"}, None)
        self.assertEqual(action["status"], "awaiting_confirmation")
        confirmed = self.actions.confirm(action["action_id"], "Yes, confirm")
        self.assertEqual(confirmed["status"], "confirmed")

    def test_unruled_device_action_is_blocked(self) -> None:
        action = self.actions.propose("owner", "device_control", "Turn off lounge lamp", {"device": "Lounge lamp"}, None)
        self.assertEqual(action["status"], "blocked")

    def test_allowlisted_device_action_is_authorized(self) -> None:
        self.actions.create_rule("lounge-lamp", "owner", "device_control", "Lounge lamp controls", ["Lounge lamp"], datetime.now(UTC) + timedelta(hours=1), True)
        action = self.actions.propose("owner", "device_control", "Turn off lounge lamp", {"device": "Lounge lamp"}, "lounge-lamp")
        self.assertEqual(action["status"], "authorized")
        other = self.actions.propose("owner", "device_control", "Turn off bedroom lamp", {"device": "Bedroom lamp"}, "lounge-lamp")
        self.assertEqual(other["status"], "blocked")

    def test_approved_action_is_not_sent_without_connector(self) -> None:
        action = self.actions.propose("owner", "message", "Send a dinner message", {"target": "Ava"}, None)
        confirmed = self.actions.confirm(action["action_id"], "confirm")
        executor = ActionExecutor(SimpleNamespace(action_webhook_url="", action_webhook_token="", request_timeout_seconds=5.0), self.database)
        outcome = asyncio.run(executor.execute(confirmed["action_id"]))
        self.assertEqual(outcome["delivery"]["status"], "connector_unavailable")

    def test_skill_registry_seeds_catalog(self) -> None:
        registry = SkillRegistry(self.database)
        registry.initialize()
        skills = {skill["skill_id"]: skill for skill in registry.list()}
        self.assertTrue(skills["web_search"]["enabled"])
        self.assertFalse(skills["google_drive"]["enabled"])

    def test_vault_saves_to_the_active_profile_folder(self) -> None:
        self.database.create_profile("alex", "Alex", True, {})
        vault = ProfileVault(SimpleNamespace(data_dir=Path(self.temp_dir.name) / "data"))
        saved = vault.save_text("alex", "Dinner options", "Try the quiet Italian restaurant.", "plans")
        self.assertTrue((Path(self.temp_dir.name) / "data" / "profiles" / saved["relative_path"]).exists())
        self.assertEqual(vault.list_files("owner"), [])
        self.assertEqual(len(vault.list_files("alex")), 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/tests/test_google_drive.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.db import Database
from app.services.google_drive import GoogleDriveMirror
from app.services.vault import ProfileVault


class GoogleDriveMirrorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temp_dir.name)
        self.drive_root = self.root / "drive-mirror"
        self.drive_root.mkdir()
        (self.drive_root / "household-notes.md").write_text("Ava prefers quiet restaurants near home.", encoding="utf-8")
        self.database = Database(self.root / "agent.db")
        self.database.initialize("Owner")
        self.settings = SimpleNamespace(data_dir=self.root / "data", google_drive_sync_dir=self.drive_root)
        self.vault = ProfileVault(self.settings)
        self.mirror = GoogleDriveMirror(self.settings, self.database, self.vault)

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        self.temp_dir.cleanup()

    def test_lists_and_imports_only_supported_text_documents(self) -> None:
        files = self.mirror.list_files()
        self.assertEqual(files[0]["relative_path"], "household-notes.md")
        result = self.mirror.import_file("owner", "household-notes.md", remember_excerpt=True)
        self.assertEqual(result["source_file"], "household-notes.md")

    def test_rejects_paths_outside_the_configured_mirror(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self.mirror.import_file("owner", "../agent.db", remember_excerpt=False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/tests/test_tasks.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

from app.db import Database
from app.services.actions import ActionPolicyService
from app.services.tasks import TaskPlanner


class FakePlannerOllama:
    async def complete(self, messages, temperature=0.0):
        return "Gather the details needed to prepare this task."


class TaskPlannerTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.database = Database(Path(self.temp_dir.name) / "agent.db")
        self.database.initialize("Owner")
        self.planner = TaskPlanner(self.database, FakePlannerOllama(), ActionPolicyService(self.database))

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        self.temp_dir.cleanup()

    def test_message_task_creates_a_confirmation_proposal(self) -> None:
        plan = asyncio.run(self.planner.plan("owner", "Send a message to Ava about dinner at 7."))
        self.assertEqual(plan["action_type"], "message")
        self.assertEqual(plan["proposal"]["status"], "awaiting_confirmation")

    def test_research_task_has_no_external_action(self) -> None:
        plan = asyncio.run(self.planner.plan("owner", "Research quiet dinner options nearby."))
        self.assertIsNone(plan["action_type"])
        self.assertIsNone(plan["proposal"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


################################################################################
FILE: agent_server/tests/validate_compose.py
################################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import yaml

repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
compose_path = repo_root / "compose.yml"
document = yaml.safe_load(compose_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
assert document["name"] == "aura-kiosk-agent"
services = document["services"]
assert {"agent-server", "searxng"}.issubset(services)
agent = services["agent-server"]
assert "searxng" in agent["depends_on"]
assert any("agent_data:/data" in volume for volume in agent["volumes"])
assert "GOOGLE_DRIVE_SYNC_DIR" in agent["environment"]
assert agent["read_only"] is True
assert "no-new-privileges:true" in agent["security_opt"]
print("Compose YAML structure validated.")

FILE: BRAIN/AI/IMAGE/decohere_ai.py
################################################################################

import requests
import base64
from typing import Tuple, Optional
import os
from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()  # Load environment variables from .env file


def generate(
    prompt: str,
    seed: int = 1800647681,
    width: int = 1024,
    height: int = 576,
    steps: int = 4,
    enhance: bool = True,
    safety_filter: bool = True,
    image_path: str = "ASSETS/image.jpg",
) -> Tuple[bool, Optional[str]]:
    """
    Generates an image based on the given parameters and saves it to a file.

    Parameters:
    - prompt (str): Description of the image to generate.
    - seed (int): Seed for the image generation process.
    - width (int): Width of the generated image.
    - height (int): Height of the generated image.
    - steps (int): Number of steps for the image generation process. More the Steps More Clear and Realistic Image
    - enhance (bool): Whether to enhance the image quality.
    - safety_filter (bool): Whether to apply a safety filter to the image.

    - For Square Image Size: 768x768
    - For Portrait Image Size: 1024x576
    - For Landscape Image Size: 576x1024

    Returns:
    - Tuple[bool, Optional[str]]: A tuple containing a boolean indicating the success of the API call,
      and an optional string with the file path where the image is saved if successful.
    """

    # Define the URL and headers for the API call
    url = "https://turbo.decohere.ai/generate/turbo"
    headers = {
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {os.environ.get('DECOHERE_AI')}",
    }

    # Define the payload for the POST request
    payload = {
        "prompt": prompt,
        "seed": seed,
        "width": width,
        "height": height,
        "steps": steps,
        "enhance": enhance,
        "safety_filter": safety_filter,
    }

    # Make the POST request to the API with timeout + error handling
    try:
        response = requests.post(url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=30)
    except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
        return False, f"Network error: {e}"
    if response.status_code == 200:
        try:
            base64_image_data = response.json().get("image", "")
            if not base64_image_data:
                return False, f"No image in response: {response.text[:200]}"
            image_bytes = base64.b64decode(base64_image_data)
            with open(image_path, "wb") as image_file:
                image_file.write(image_bytes)
            return True, image_path
        except Exception as e:
            return False, f"Decode failed: {e} – {response.text[:200]}"
    else:
        return False, f"API {response.status_code}: {response.text[:200]}"


# Example usage:
